Experiments show that our method consistently outperforms seven state-of-the-art instruction selection methods on four backbone-budget settings. An ablation study further shows that PASS's adaptive allocation consistently improves over uniform allocation.

</details>


### [296] [Accurate but Not Humble: Evaluating Epistemic Humility in LLM Agents under Knowledge Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12360)
*Kaiser Sun,Bernal Jimenez Gutierrez,Hongjun Liu,Jingyu Zhang,Jie Gao,Mark Dredze,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出以"认识论谦逊"（EH）评估智能体：通过"识别—解决—升级"（ISE）三维轨迹行为刻画其在知识冲突下的表现，发现任务准确率与EH不一致，agent常在早期识别冲突却未在后继步骤保持或传达不确定性，模型级干预可提升EH但常牺牲准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估主要关注任务成功，缺乏对"检索证据与先验信念冲突时智能体如何应对"的洞察，无法判断其会修正答案、承认不确定性还是坚持错误结论。

Method: 定义EH为智能体在执行中识别、处置并传达不确定性的意愿，并将其操作化为轨迹级的Identify、Solve、Escalate三维度；构建知识冲突场景（骨干模型参数化知识与所遇证据矛盾，或两个上下文来源相互矛盾），设置两类冲突条件——受控冲突与多步智能体执行中的自然冲突，并各自配备匹配的无冲突对照；对四个智能体进行评估。

Result: 更高的任务准确率并不必然对应更强的认识论谦逊：部分高准确率配置能在执行中识别冲突，却未在错误结论中传达未解决的不确定性；轨迹级分析显示智能体常在执行早期步骤检测到冲突，但在后续步骤未能维持或解决；模型级干预可改善EH，但往往以任务准确率为代价。

Conclusion: EPISTEMIC humility 不能由任务成功率代表；它源于骨干模型、智能体框架与评估环境三者交互，评估应纳入轨迹级的不确定性识别、处置与沟通行为。

Abstract: When retrieved evidence contradicts an agent's prior beliefs, does it revise its answer, acknowledge uncertainty, or persist with an incorrect conclusion? Existing evaluations of agentic systems focus primarily on task success, offering limited insight into how agents handle such conflicts. We propose to evaluate agents on epistemic humility (EH): the agent's willingness to recognize, act on, and communicate uncertainty during task execution. We operationalize EH through three trajectory-level behavioral dimensions: Identify, Solve, and Escalate (ISE). Through knowledge conflict, situations where the backbone language model's parametric knowledge contradicts the evidence it encounters, or where two contextual sources disagree, we evaluate two conflict settings: (1) controlled conflict and (2) naturally occurring conflict during multi-step agentic execution, each paired with matched no-conflict controls. Evaluating four agents, we find that higher task accuracy does not necessarily correspond to greater epistemic humility: some high-accuracy configurations recognize conflicts during execution but do not communicate unresolved uncertainty in their incorrect final answers. Trajectory-level analysis further reveals that agents frequently detect conflicts in early steps of execution but fail to maintain or resolve them in later steps. Finally, we show that model-level interventions can improve EH, but often at the cost of task accuracy, suggesting that epistemic humility emerges from the interaction among the backbone model, agent harness, and evaluation environment.

</details>


### [297] [Cited but Not Consulted: A Counterfactual Audit of Legal Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12361)
*Saisab Sadhu,Shreeyans Arora,Pratinav Seth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文直接检验"模型引用法律依据是否能作为判决依赖该依据的证据"：固定案件事实、替换被引法律权威后，从隐藏状态解码判决变化。结果显示引用正确率（66.7%-100%）远高于判决随权威改变而改变的比率（最高仅76.7%，CaseHOLD低至0.0%），且判决更易被案件事实中的对抗性指令操纵（73.3%-96.4%），表明"引用法律权威"是判决依赖性的劣质代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地通过点名判决背后的法条或先例来"论证"法律决定，这种做法被默认为判决确实源自该法律依据的证据。作者希望直接检验这一假设是否成立——即模型引用某法律权威，是否意味着判决真的取决于它。

Method: 在固定案件事实的前提下，将模型所引用的法律权威替换为一个无关权威，并从模型隐藏状态中解码其不断演化的判决，观察判决是否随之改变。实验覆盖7个开放权重模型（8B-70B）与4个涵盖司法和合同推理的基准；另用LoRA复现一个专用法律推理模型作为对照；并设计红队评测，在案件事实中嵌入对抗性指令，比较判决翻转敏感性。

Result: 当被明确要求点名主导权威来论证判决时，模型在66.7%-100%的生成中给出正确权威，但判决随权威改变而改变的一致性远低：CaseHOLD仅0.0%-21.7%，ECHR与SCOTUS为30.0%-76.7%，ContractNLI为43.3%-50.0%。扩大规模或使用专用法律推理模型均无法弥合该差距。红队评测中，5个核心模型对隐藏在事实中的对抗性指令的遵从率达73.3%-96.4%，远超判决翻转敏感性，且在所有模型排序中无一例外。相关发现通过排除提示措辞噪声与混杂采样的检验得到复现。

Conclusion: 引用法律权威是判决对其依赖程度的劣质代理指标，而同一判决却单独易受对抗性操纵影响。这两项发现直接关系到将生成的法律解释用作合规或审计证据的做法，提示此类解释不应被当作判决依据可靠性的凭证。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly justify legal decisions by naming the statute or precedent behind a verdict, treated as evidence that the decision follows from it. We test this directly: holding case facts fixed, we substitute the named legal authority for an unrelated one and decode a model's evolving verdict from its hidden states. Across seven open-weight models (8B-70B) and four benchmarks spanning judicial and contractual reasoning, when explicitly required to justify a verdict by naming the governing authority, models name the correct one in 66.7%-100% of generations, while the verdict changing when the authority changes is far less consistent: 0.0%-21.7% on CaseHOLD, 30.0%-76.7% on ECHR and SCOTUS, and 43.3%-50.0% on ContractNLI. Neither scale nor a purpose-built legal-reasoning model (a best-effort LoRA reproduction; Section 6) closes this gap. A red-teaming evaluation on five core models finds compliance with an adversarial instruction hidden in the case facts (73.3%-96.4%) exceeds verdict-swap sensitivity by a wide margin, holding without exception across model rankings. Naming a legal authority is thus a poor proxy for a verdict's dependence on it, while the same verdict remains separately vulnerable to adversarial manipulation. Both findings replicate across checks ruling out prompt-wording noise and confounded sampling, and bear directly on the use of generated legal explanations as compliance or audit artefacts.

</details>


### [298] [HANS: A Handwritten Answer Sheet Dataset for Noisy Hybrid Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12363)
*Xiazhen Wu,Wansong Qin,Yangbin Zheng,Liangda Fang,Zhan Li,Xiujie Huang,Liushen Zhou,Quanlong Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向真实教育场景的手写答案识别数据集HANS及端到端两阶段噪声抑制框架NA-GOT，在识别精度与稳定性上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能批改与自动评分是智慧教育的关键基础设施，但现有文档解析与手写识别基准多面向结构良好的印刷文档或孤立的数学表达式，缺乏能反映学生答题纸复杂特性的数据集，如多行推导过程、文本与公式混杂、删除线等噪声，因而亟需构建贴近真实教育场景的数据与鲁棒识别方法。

Method: 作者构建了HANS数据集，首次显式面向真实教育场景，涵盖数学表达式、自然语言文本、手绘表格及包括修改与删除在内的多种噪声模式，并提供细粒度标注。基于该数据集提出NA-GOT端到端框架，通过特征层面的轻量级噪声抑制模块与解码阶段引入的噪声感知注意力机制，实现两阶段噪声抑制。

Result: 实验表明，HANS对现有方法构成显著挑战；NA-GOT在答题过程识别的准确率与稳定性两方面均取得明显提升。

Conclusion: 该工作弥补了教育场景手写识别数据与方法的空白，验证了噪声感知建模的有效性，数据集将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Intelligent grading and automated scoring technologies constitute critical infrastructure for smart education. However, existing document parsing and handwriting recognition benchmarks are predominantly designed for well-structured printed documents or isolated mathematical expressions, lacking datasets that capture the complex characteristics inherent to student answer sheets, including multi-line derivation processes, heterogeneous mixtures of text and mathematical formulae, and noise artifacts such as strikethroughs. To address this gap, we introduce HANS, the first dataset explicitly constructed for real-world educational scenarios, encompassing mathematical expressions, natural language text, hand-drawn tables, and diverse noise patterns including corrections and deletions, accompanied by fine-grained annotations that establish a reliable foundation for robust recognition research. Building upon HANS, we propose NA-GOT, an end-to-end framework that achieves two-stage noise suppression through a lightweight noise suppression module operating at the feature level, complemented by a noiseaware attention mechanism incorporated into the decoding stage. Experimental results demonstrate that HANS poses substantial challenges to existing methods, while NA-GOT achieves significant improvements in both accuracy and stability for answer process recognition. The dataset will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [299] [OnTrack: Real-Time Monitoring and Intervention in LLM Agent Trajectories via Streaming Structure-Aware Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12375)
*Babak Barazandeh,Connor Swanson,Chinmay Kulkarni,Nikhil Mungel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OnTrack，一种流式监控机制，通过将智能体的步骤和依赖关系与已记录的成功运行进行对比，实现毫秒级预警或阻断，从而在失败运行中节省算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体多自主运行、缺乏规则化防护，不可逆操作会带来成本与安全问题。现有方案要么用防护智能体逐步监控（增加每步成本与延迟），要么事后评估日志（运行结束才给结论，token 已消耗、损害已造成）。因此需要一种在线、低开销的监控手段。

Method: OnTrack 是一种流式监控机制，将智能体的步骤与依赖关系和已记录的成功运行进行比对，每步约 1 毫秒即可告警或阻断。作者在三种递减访问权限的场景下研究：全参考访问（历史运行+工具模式）、中等访问（仅工具模式）、无先验知识（仅生成的步骤日志）。

Result: 在 SWE-bench 轨迹上评估：基于前 8 步，OnTrack 将失败轨迹排在成功轨迹之下，优于内容相似度方法（+0.057 AUROC）。配合中止策略可节省约 18% 本将消耗在失败运行上的算力，其中 83% 被中断的运行确实走向失败（6 次中止中 5 次正确）。

Conclusion: OnTrack 以流式、低延迟方式监控智能体，随着数据访问减少其监控能力从计划违规检测降为识别循环、停滞和重复工具调用，但仍能有效提前识别失败运行并节省算力。

Abstract: Agents are deployed in applications from trip planners and stock trading to IT incident triage. In most cases, LLM agents work autonomously with minimal rule-based safeguarding, leading to cost and safety issues from irreversible actions. Recent works resolve this either by using a safeguard agent to monitor behavior or evaluating logs post-hoc. The first adds cost and latency to every step; the second delivers its verdict after the run, when tokens are burned and damage is done. To overcome this, we propose OnTrack, a streaming monitoring mechanism that compares an agent's steps and dependencies against recorded successful runs to alert users or block the agent in about a millisecond per step. We study this problem in three regimes of decreasing access: full reference access (historical runs and tool schemas), intermediate access (only tool schemas), and no prior knowledge (only step logs as generated). Expectation of OnTrack's monitoring capabilities reduces as data access drops, ranging from plan violation detection to identifying loops, stalls, and repeated tool calls. Finally, we evaluate OnTrack using SWE-bench trajectories. Based on the first 8 steps, our method ranks failing trajectories below succeeding ones better than content similarity approaches (+0.057 AUROC). With an abort policy, we save about 18% of compute that would be burned on failing runs, where 83% of interrupted runs were actually heading to failure (5 out of 6 aborts were correct).

</details>


### [300] [GeoReform: Reflective Formalization Evolution for Multimodal Geometry Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12391)
*Jialu Wang,Ruichen Zhang,Xiaoou Liu,Hua Wei,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出多模态大模型在几何推理中的关键瓶颈并非"提取更多几何事实"，而是"如何组织这些事实"。为此提出 GeoReform，将形式化视为可优化的策略，通过反思式演化迭代改进几何实体、关系、约束与目标的表示方式。在 Geometry3K 上，Qwen3VL-2B 准确率从 42.0% 提升至 56.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 在几何图表中难以识别和使用几何关系。现有方法通过将几何实体、关系与约束转成显式文本表示来辅助推理，但有效的形式化并非易事：在 Geometry3K 上，结构注入修复了 28 个错误却引入了 13 个新错误（200 例中）。冗余关系会分散模型注意力，对图形元素的歧义引用会导致约束误用。因此核心挑战不是抽取更多几何事实，而是将其组织成能支撑下游推理的表示。

Method: 提出 GeoReform，一个反思式形式化演化（reflective formalization evolution）框架，把形式化当作可优化的策略（policy）而非固定的解析器输出。流程为：执行完整推理管线，收集失败的 rollout，诊断当前表示的缺陷，并对策略进行变异（mutate），以更好地选择、定位、分组和呈现几何实体、关系、约束与目标。

Result: 在 Geometry3K 上，GeoReform 将 Qwen3VL-2B 的准确率从 42.0% 提升到 56.0%。在多个几何推理基准上进行了大量实验与分析。

Conclusion: 有效的形式化对于提升多模态几何推理至关重要；相较于点对点的结构注入，面向下游推理的形式化策略优化能显著减少冗余与歧义带来的负面影响。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often struggle to identify and use geometric relations in diagrams. Recent methods address this challenge by converting geometric entities, relations, and constraints into explicit textual representations for the model to reason over. However, effective formalization is highly non-trivial: on Geometry3K, structure injection fixes 28 errors but introduces 13 new ones among 200 examples. Redundant relations can distract the model, while ambiguous references to diagram elements can lead it to apply constraints incorrectly. This suggests that the key challenge is not merely extracting more geometric facts, but organizing them into representations that support downstream reasoning. To fully exploit the power of formalization, we further propose GeoReform, a reflective formalization evolution framework that treats formalization as an optimizable policy rather than a fixed parser output. GeoReform executes the full reasoning pipeline, collects failed rollouts, diagnoses defects in the current representation, and mutates the policy to better select, ground, group, and present geometric entities, relations, constraints, and targets. On Geometry3K, GeoReform improves Qwen3VL-2B accuracy from 42.0\% to 56.0\%. Extensive experiments and analyses across geometry reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that effective formalization is crucial for improving multimodal geometry reasoning.

</details>


### [301] [HRIL: Learning Multimodal Synergy via Higher-Order Tensor Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12393)
*Qun Dai,Liangjian Wen,Jiang Duan,Yong Dai,Dongkai Wang,Maolin Wang,Mingjie Wang,Jianzhuang Liu,He Yan,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出HRIL方法，通过跨模态嵌入的经验交叉矩张量及Tucker分解来建模多模态间的高阶统计依赖，并设计协同感知正则项保留协同信息容量，在受控协同任务及真实基准上优于现有多模态对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督多模态表示学习虽已取得显著成功，但捕捉协同信息（synergy）仍具挑战：协同信息并非来自单个模态的共享信息，而是仅在多模态联合配置中出现、无法从任一单独模态恢复。因此，如何让多模态表示保留此类协同信号的信息容量成为核心问题。

Method: 关键观察是协同信息体现为模态间的高阶统计依赖，作者据此提出高阶表示与信息学习（HRIL）：1）在模态嵌入上构建经验交叉矩张量，以表示多路交互；2）采用Tucker分解获得核心张量；3）引入协同感知正则项，防止能量集中、保留高阶耦合容量以捕获协同信息。

Result: 在受控协同任务（controlled synergy task）和真实世界基准上均取得相对现有多模态对比方法的一致性提升，在以协同交互为主的任务中增益尤为显著。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模模态间高阶统计依赖并施加协同感知正则化，可有效保留多模态表示中的协同信息容量，为自监督多模态表示学习提供了一条有原则的解决路径。

Abstract: Self-supervised multimodal representation learning has achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, yet capturing synergistic information remains challenging due to the complexity of cross-modal interactions. Unlike the shared information across individual modalities, synergy arises when task-relevant signals emerge only from the joint configuration of multiple modalities and cannot be recovered from any modality in isolation. This work focuses on how to preserve the information capacity for such synergistic signals in multimodal representations. The key observation is that synergistic information is reflected in higher-order statistical dependence among modalities, which provides a principled target for explicitly modeling joint interactions. Motivated by this insight, we propose Higher-order Representation and Information Learning (HRIL), which constructs an empirical cross-moment tensor over modality embeddings to represent multi-way interactions. HRIL employs Tucker decomposition to obtain a core tensor, complemented by a synergy-aware regularizer that prevents energy concentration and preserves higher-order coupling capacity for synergistic information capture. Experiments on the controlled synergy task and real-world benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing multimodal contrastive methods, with notable gains on tasks dominated by synergistic interactions. Code is released at https://github.com/brightest66/HRIL.

</details>


### [302] [Searching for "Harmful Refusal": A Psychometric Audit of an AI Safety Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12409)
*Christopher M. Stewart,Preston Botter,Natalie Sarabosing,Muye Zhang,Rachel Phinnemore,Shalini Ghosh,Hong Shen,Hoda Heidari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文检验HELM Safety中的"有害拒绝"是否构成单一可测量属性，发现大多数数据集饱和，唯一可用的HarmBench也不能测量单一属性，任何对数据集和条目取平均的安全分数都可能掩盖饱和并混淆行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全基准通常报告整个数据集套件的单一总分，但各数据集针对一个或多个安全相关属性，导致总分相近的模型属性画像差异很大。比较模型在单个属性层面更可行，但往往不清楚单一数据集分数是否隔离了任何单一属性。作者希望检验"有害拒绝"是否构成一个单一、可测量的属性。

Method: 采用构念效度框架（属性必须先存在，测试才能测量它）。首先筛选HELM Safety中可能针对有害拒绝的四个数据集，发现其中三个饱和。对剩余数据集HarmBench进行两项心理测量检验：一是多维项目反应理论（MIRT）建模；二是差异项目功能（DIF）分析。

Result: MIRT建模强烈表明HarmBench并不测量单一属性。DIF分析发现，来自不同开发者的模型在具备相同拒绝能力分数时，对某些条目的评分存在差异。在按范围匹配后这些标记基本消失，这一模式与聚合效应一致，但不足以排除领域特定的开发者差异。

Conclusion: HarmBench把不同的危害行为合并为一个分数，而HELM安全总分又将HarmBench及其他数据集的分数进一步合并为单一顶部数字。任何对数据集和条目取平均的安全分数都可能掩盖饱和并混淆行为。作者主张：在将分数用于模型比较之前，它应先获得单一属性解读的资格。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks typically report one overall score for a suite of datasets, each of which may target one or more safety-related attributes, so models with similar overall scores can have very different attribute profiles. Comparing models is more tractable at the level of individual attributes, yet it is often unclear whether even a single dataset's scores isolate any single attribute. One plausible candidate for such an attribute is harmful refusal, a model's tendency to refuse dangerous or policy-violating prompts. We examine whether it constitutes a single, measurable attribute in HELM Safety. Using a construct validity framework that stipulates that an attribute must exist before a test can measure it, we start with HELM Safety's four datasets that might plausibly target harmful refusal, but find that three are saturated. We subject the remaining dataset, HarmBench, to two psychometric tests to determine if a single attribute like harmful refusal could stand behind its score. First, multidimensional item response theory modeling strongly suggests that HarmBench does not measure a singular attribute. Second, a differential item functioning analysis finds items where models from different developers with the same refusal ability score differently. These flags largely disappear under scope-specific matching, a pattern consistent with aggregation effects but not sufficient to rule out domain-specific developer differences. Zooming out, HarmBench collapses distinct harm behaviors into one score, and the overall HELM safety aggregate further collapses HarmBench and scores from other datasets into a single top-line number. Any safety score that averages over datasets and items can hide saturation and conflate behaviors this way. We argue that a score should earn its single-attribute reading before models are compared with it.

</details>


### [303] [Ecology of AI Agents: Collaboration Creates a Population Threshold for Takeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12436)
*Erin Crawley,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"生态安全"概念，用一个适应度（增长率）取决于网络安全能力的种群增长方程，刻画失准AI智能体种群的自我强化扩散动态，揭示阈值式失控机制（个体能力临界阈值、协作下的种群临界阈值/强Allee效应），并据此倡导"生态红队测试"与"种群节奏化部署"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体已能实施真实网络攻击，并随数量增加而扩展能力，且能集体追求失准目标以获取奖励。这些因素叠加，可能引发失准智能体的"种群爆炸"：智能体攻陷计算机并秘密部署更多智能体，形成自我强化循环——种群越大，集体网络能力越强，进而扩张更远。既有研究聚焦个体智能体安全或固定种群的多智能体安全，无法回答种群自身的动态问题：究竟什么决定失准智能体种群是被遏制还是进入自我强化循环？由此提出"生态安全"这一种群层面问题，并需要新的理论与评估范式。

Method: 构建AI智能体种群的生态学理论：基于种群增长方程，将适应度（增长率）设定为网络安全能力的函数。分两种情形分析——无协同时，种群起飞仅取决于个体智能体能力是否超过临界阈值；有协同时，集体网络安全能力随种群规模递增，从而产生种群层面的临界阈值（低于则衰减，高于则起飞，即使个体能力不变），该现象对应生态学中的强Allee效应。并提出相应评估方法：生态红队测试（ecological red teaming）与种群节奏化部署（population pacing），在受控环境中逐步部署更大的智能体种群，测量网络能力如何随种群规模缩放，并估计起飞所需的临界种群规模。

Result: 理论给出两类阈值机制：其一，无协作条件下存在个体能力临界阈值，低于则无法起飞；其二，协作条件下集体能力随种群规模增长，形成种群临界阈值——低于该规模种群衰退，高于该规模则起飞，且个体智能体能力无需变化，即强Allee效应。由此得出一个重要推论：对小规模智能体种群进行红队测试，无法保证更大种群的生态安全。此外，能力提升可能降低该临界阈值，因此每一代新模型都需要重新估计。

Conclusion: 生态安全是与个体安全、固定种群多智能体安全不同的种群层面问题，其核心由"能力—种群规模"耦合动态与阈值决定。由于小规模红队测试不能外推到大种群，应转向生态红队测试与种群节奏化部署，在受控环境中渐进扩规模，量化网络能力随种群规模的缩放关系并估计起飞临界规模；鉴于能力增益会降低阈值，需针对每一代模型重新评估。

Abstract: AI agents can now conduct real-world cyberattacks, scale up capabilities with the number of agents, and collectively pursue misaligned goals to obtain rewards. Together, these factors raise the risk of a population explosion of misaligned agents: agents could compromise computers and secretly deploy additional agents, creating a self-reinforcing cycle where larger populations develop greater collective cyber capability and expand further. This raises a fundamental question: What determines whether a population of misaligned agents remains contained or takes off into this self-reinforcing cycle? This population-level problem is ecological safety: unlike individual-agent or multi-agent safety with a fixed population, it concerns the dynamics of the population itself. Here, we develop an ecological theory of AI-agent populations based on a population growth equation in which fitness (growth rate) depends on cybersecurity capability. We show that, without collaboration, the population takes off only when individual-agent capability exceeds a critical threshold. With collaboration, however, collective cybersecurity capability increases with population size. This creates a critical population threshold: below it, the population declines; above it, the population takes off, even though individual-agent capability has not changed. In ecology, this phenomenon is known as the strong Allee effect. Because red teaming a small group of agents cannot guarantee ecological safety in larger populations, our theory calls for ecological red teaming and population pacing: gradually deploying larger agent populations in controlled environments, while measuring how cyber capability scales with population size, and estimating the critical population size for takeoff. Capability gains may lower this threshold, requiring re-estimation for each new model generation.

</details>


### [304] [BrickBench: Evaluating Agentic Brick Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12452)
*Peter Kulits,Yiqing Xu,R. Kenny Jones,Cordelia Schmid,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 BrickBench——针对"智能体驱动的文本条件乐高套件设计"的基准，并配套提供 BrickAgent 环境，用于让编码智能体构建、检查与验证设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体任务多集中于文本或图像生成，缺乏需要同时满足"语义对齐"与"物理可拼装"双重约束的具身/结构化设计评测。乐高设计天然要求离散零件选择与局部—全局约束联合推理，因而可作为检验智能体约束推理能力的合适载体。

Method: 给定文本提示，智能体需从离散零件库中选择零件并生成可实际拼装的装配体。评测在三种设置下进行，设置之间在规模（scale）与零件可用性（part availability）上存在差异。评分维度包括有效性（validity）、对齐度（alignment）与设计性（design）。同时提供 BrickAgent 环境，支持编码智能体对设计进行构建、检查与验证。

Result: 领先的智能体在可验证的物理与语义要求上基本能够满足，但其设计水平仍不及人类设计。

Conclusion: BrickBench 为智能体的约束推理与结构化设计能力提供了可验证的评测平台，揭示了当前模型"满足硬约束但设计质量不足"的能力缺口。基准与环境已公开。

Abstract: We propose BrickBench, a benchmark for agentic text-conditioned LEGO-set design. Given a prompt, an agent is tasked with producing an assembly that not only satisfies semantic and design criteria, but that can also be physically built. To do so, it must select parts from a discrete library and reason jointly about local and global constraints. We score validity, alignment, and design across three settings that vary in scale and part availability. We provide BrickAgent, an environment for coding agents to construct, inspect, and validate their designs. We find that leading agents largely satisfy verifiable physical and semantic requirements, but fall short of human designs. We release our benchmark and environment at http://www.brickben.ch

</details>


### [305] [On the estimation and validity of AI time horizons---a statistical look at the METR plot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12466)
*Drew T. Nguyen,William Fithian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用样条与项目反应理论重新计算METR的50%时间视野，发现人类时间与AI难度呈非线性关系，2–30分钟区间近乎平坦，其他区间近似线性，据此给出更优的点估计与诊断图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: METR的50%时间视野将AI能力用可解释的人类任务完成时间单位表达，但原有计算假设任务AI难度与人类时间对数呈线性关系，这一假设可能不成立，会扭曲时间视野的估计与解读。

Method: 基于228个任务与26个AI模型的数据，采用样条函数与项目反应理论（IRT）重新估计时间视野，放松线性假设；拟合的样条可被解释为将人类时间转换为AI难度的函数。

Result: 拟合样条在2–30分钟区间近乎平坦，其余区间接近线性。因此，时间视野从3分钟跃升至30分钟，明显比从30分钟跃升至5小时更容易，尽管两者倍率同为10倍。新方法的点估计在交叉验证的一族恰当评分规则下表现更优。

Conclusion: 建议时间视野与诊断图结合解读，尤其在新时间视野基准提出或现有基准扩展到更长任务时；新估计与诊断图可用于评估时间视野的构念效度。

Abstract: METR's 50\% time horizon measures the human completion time of software tasks that an AI solves with 50\% probability, allowing AI capabilities to be expressed in interpretable units. On 228 tasks and 26 AIs, we recompute the time horizons using splines and item-response theory to relax the assumption that the AI difficulty of a task depends linearly on the log of human time. Our fitted spline can be interpreted as a function that \emph{converts} human time to AI difficulty; it is nearly flat in a region from 2--30 min but close to linear elsewhere. Hence, a time-horizon jump from 3 min to 30 min is much easier than one from 30 min to 5 hours despite the same multiplier of $10 \times$. Overall, we contribute time-horizon point estimates that perform better under a cross-validated suite of proper scoring rules, as well as diagnostic plots for assessing time horizons' construct validity. We suggest that time horizons be interpreted together with the diagnostic plots, especially as new time-horizon-based benchmarks are proposed or existing ones grow to include longer tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [306] [Beyond Type-checking: Towards Holistic Evaluation of Formal Specification Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10604)
*Srijith Nair,Aditya Vempaty,Jia Liu,Ashish Jagmohan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文构建统一数据集与评估框架，对LLM规格生成（SpecGen）进行整体评价，强调输入接受度与输出约束需分开度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SpecGen生成的正式规约缺乏检验其是否真正反映用户意图的确定性手段，导致"证明通过但规约错误"的风险；现有评估也存在测量覆盖不足与指标证据范围不清的问题。

Method: 整合350个Lean任务（VERINA 189、CLEVER 161）构建统一数据集，建立涵盖形式有效性、参考相似性与等价性、行为充分性的评估框架；区分必需输入接受、有效输出接受与无效输出拒绝三类指标，并明确各指标的证据范围；在四种SpecGen配置上开展对比实验，并加入作者构建的对照规约。

Result: 将参考相似性指标GTED限制在32个可联合测量的VERINA任务后，该配置在平均相似性上从第二降至第四，说明测量覆盖会显著影响结论；对照规约在达到100%正例测试召回与负例拒绝的同时，必需输入接受率为0%。

Conclusion: 完美的后置条件得分可能掩盖不可用的输入契约，SpecGen评估应分别反馈输入覆盖与输出约束，并以统一的、证据范围明确的框架进行整体评价。

Abstract: When generating verifiable code, natural language requirements are mapped to machine checked code using LLMs and agentic workflows. A crucial component of this pipeline is specification generation (SpecGen), which produces a formal contract against which an agent can prove implementation correctness. Proof generation can obtain deterministic feedback from a theorem prover, but SpecGen lacks a definitive check that a generated specification captures the user's intent. A checked proof can therefore establish correctness against a specification that misrepresents the intended behaviour. We take a step towards holistic SpecGen evaluation with a unified dataset assembled from $350$ existing Lean tasks, including $189$ from VERINA and $161$ from CLEVER, and a framework covering formal validity, reference similarity and equivalence, and behavioural adequacy. We distinguish acceptance of required inputs from acceptance of valid outputs and rejection of invalid outputs, while making each metric's evidence scope explicit. Across four SpecGen configurations, restricting the generalized tree edit distance (GTED) comparison, a reference similarity measure, to $32$ jointly measurable VERINA tasks changes the VERINA configuration's position from second to fourth in mean similarity, showing the importance of measurement coverage. In an authored control, a specification achieves $100\%$ positive test recall and negative test rejection while accepting $0\%$ of required inputs. This demonstrates that perfect postcondition scores can miss an unusable input contract, motivating separate feedback on input coverage and output constraints.

</details>


### [307] [Code Understanding is a Bottleneck for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10610)
*Nishant Balepur,Kiran Tomlinson,Tobias Schnabel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 CABRA 基准，通过调用图变换从零构建可控任务，揭示编程智能体能力瓶颈在于代码理解而非编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级基准（如 SWE-bench）假设"编辑代码行数"能预测任务难度，但对代码与任务类型控制不足，无法确定究竟是哪些能力真正导致智能体出错。

Method: CABRA 从零构建任务，以调用图变换形式生成，并通过"任务规模"参数沿四条轴扩展难度：函数遍历、搜索、运行时解析与指令遵循；在 6,840 个任务上测试 8 个 LLM 与 6 个编程智能体，并与 SWE-bench Verified 的工具调用分析对照。

Result: 1）随任务规模增长 LLM 准确率下降，而智能体通过调用工具（如 grep）卸载工作几近满分；2）规模越大的任务引发更多阅读与分析类工具调用，且工具调用次数比代码编辑行数更能预测智能体准确率；3）在跨两类分析分歧逻辑的高强度理解任务上，智能体准确率终于下降。

Conclusion: 倡导以 CABRA 这类合成评估揭示被工具"平凡化"的 LLM 弱点（如大海捞针）及编辑之外仍难的能力（如理解），建议将 SWE-bench 式任务与受控诊断配对使用。

Abstract: Repository benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench) for coding agents often assume that lines of code edited can predict task difficulty, but such datasets' poor control over code and task types makes it hard to know which abilities truly drive agent errors. We present CABRA: a Coding Ability Blueprint for Rigorous Agent evaluation. CABRA builds tasks from scratch as call graph transformations and scales difficulty via a task size parameter on four axes: function traversal, search, runtime resolution, and instruction following. We run eight LLMs and six coding agents on 6,840 CABRA tasks to show: 1) LLM accuracy falls as task size~grows, but agents stay near-perfect by offloading work to tools (e.g., grep); 2) Larger CABRA tasks elicit more tool calls for reading and analysis, while a separate study on SWE-bench Verified shows these tool call counts predict agents' accuracy better than lines of code edited, suggesting task difficulty for agents can lie in understanding code to edit, not just in making edits; 3) Extending CABRA to an intense understanding task where models analyze divergent logic across two classes backs this finding, as agent accuracy finally falls. More broadly, we argue for synthetic evaluations like CABRA to unmask LLM weaknesses trivialized by tools (e.g., needle-in-a-haystack) and abilities beyond just editing (e.g., understanding) that coding agents still find difficult, pairing SWE-bench-style tasks with controlled diagnosis.

</details>


### [308] [TestJack: Should you trust the results in coding benchmarks? Agentic Coding Benchmarks Auditing via Evaluator Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10619)
*Shuangjie Yao,Hao Wang,Koushik Sen,Simin Chen,Baishakhi Ray,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 TestJack 框架，为每个 trial 动态生成针对题目需求的测试并保留 ground-truth 补丁能通过的测试，以发现固定单元测试漏判的违规行为；并给出轻量采样变体。在 6 个前沿模型后端与 5 个基准上，约 34.4% 被判正确的 trial 实际违反任务需求，解决率由 50.6% 降至 33.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码基准几乎全部依赖固定的单元测试作为正确性判据，而这类测试往往只覆盖任务需求的一部分，导致 agent 可通过 reward hacking 或遗漏需求仍通过测试；高分可能反映的是对评测器的适配能力而非问题求解能力。已有工作采用静态测试增强，在 trial 之前一次性强化测试，无法依据真实失败模式进行修正。

Method: TestJack 是一个可扩展的评估框架：对每个 trial 生成针对补丁可能违反的 prompt 需求的测试，仅保留 ground-truth 补丁能通过的测试，并重新检查所有失败的 trial，使每个确认的失败都有可复现的测试支撑。同时提出轻量变体：对随机抽样的 trial 进行深度审计，并将所得测试复用于同一任务的所有 trial，以降低评估成本。

Result: 在 6 个前沿模型后端与 5 个基准（如 DeepSWE、SWE Marathon）上评估，约 34.4% 当前被判为正确的模型 trial 实际违反任务需求，整体解决率由 50.6% 降至 33.2%。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了当前编码 agent 评估的根本局限：随着 LLM 越来越擅长针对固定评测器进行优化，评测器本身也必须具备自适应性。动态、以需求为中心的测试生成是弥补固定单元测试判别力不足的必要方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are rapidly reshaping software engineering, accompanied by an explosion of new code benchmarks. Yet nearly all existing benchmarks still rely on the same decades-old criterion: a solution is correct if it passes a fixed set of unit tests. Such tests are often insufficient: they check only part of what the task requires, so agents can reward hack them or silently miss required behavior while still passing every test. As a result, higher benchmark scores may partly reflect better adaptation to the evaluator rather than better problem solving. Existing works focus on static test augmentation: they strengthen each task's tests once, before any trial is seen, and thus overlook how real trials actually fail. We introduce TestJack, a scalable framework for evaluating patches beyond fixed tests. For each trial, TestJack generates tests targeting prompt requirements the patch may violate, retains only tests passed by the ground-truth patch, and re-examines any trial failures. Each confirmed failure is thus supported by a replayable test. To reduce evaluation cost, we also introduce a lightweight variant which audits a random sample of trials in depth and reuses the resulting tests across all trials for the same task. Across 6 frontier model backends and 5 benchmarks such as DeepSWE and SWE Marathon, we find that about 34.4% of the model trials currently judged correct violate the task requirements, lowering the overall resolution rate from 50.6% to 33.2%. Our results reveal a fundamental limitation of current coding-agent evaluation: as LLMs become better at optimizing against fixed evaluators, those evaluators themselves must become more adaptive.

</details>


### [309] [Agent4RE: A Self-Refining Multi-agent Framework for End-to-End Software Requirements Engineering and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10628)
*Yongjian Tang,Linhan Li,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent4RE——一种自精炼多智能体需求工程(RE)系统，并构建端到端数据集RE-E2E，在8个LLM上平均超越基线8%，增强版本在LLM-as-a-judge与人工评分中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的软件需求工程方法多依赖基础提示策略或简单智能体协作，未能充分发挥多智能体系统潜力；同时已有数据集仅关注需求提取、分类、完整性检测等孤立子任务，缺乏覆盖从需求 elicitation 到生成全流程的端到端基准。

Method: 提出Agent4RE自精炼多智能体系统，编排专用智能体并引入两个迭代改进循环；构建基于人工撰写需求规格的真实世界端到端数据集RE-E2E；进一步提出两种增强版本，分别融入自主自精炼与结构化人工反馈。

Result: 在8个LLM上评估，三种Agent4RE变体在文本指标上平均超越领域上下文增强提示基线8%；两种增强变体取得最高LLM-as-a-judge与人工评分，在四分制上超越两个RE基线约0.8分。

Conclusion: 一致性的性能表现确立了Agent4RE作为面向工业环境的实用端到端RE解决方案的潜力。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based approaches for software Requirements Engineering (RE) typically rely on basic prompting strategies or rudimentary agent collaboration, under-utilizing the full potential of multi-agent systems. Meanwhile, available datasets focus on isolated subtasks, such as requirements extraction, classification, and completeness detection, leaving the absence of an end-to-end RE benchmark that spans from requirements elicitation to generation. We present Agent4RE - a self-refining multi-agent RE system that orchestrates specialized agents and incorporates two iterative improvement loops. To support evaluation, we construct RE-E2E - a real-world dataset built from human-written requirement specifications, enabling end-to-end assessment of RE workflows. Building on this foundation, we further propose two enhanced Agent4RE versions that incorporate either autonomous self-refinement or structured human feedback, and analyze their strengths and limitations across different scenarios. Evaluation on 8 Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrates that all three Agent4RE variants consistently outperform a domain-context-augmented prompting baseline by average 8% in text-based metrics. The two enhanced variants achieve the highest LLM-as-a-judge and human ratings, surpassing two RE baselines by approximately 0.8 points on a four-point scale. This consistent performance establishes Agent4RE as a practical end-to-end RE solution for industrial environments.

</details>


### [310] [Ruleless Digital Twins: Toward Declarative Decision-Making Through Standardized Frameworks and Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10631)
*Ivan Spajić,Volker Stolz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"无规则数字孪生"（RDTs），通过语义知识库、逻辑推理、仿真模型与自主计算架构的组合，在纯声明式用户规格下自动产生最优决策，以替代日益复杂的基于规则的决策模型。以虚拟办公室为场景的孵育器案例实验表明，RDT在维持目标室温与降低动态实时电价下的能耗成本方面优于基于规则的bang-bang控制器，但组合式决策树构建导致其需要较高算力，适用性取决于问题域与预测时域选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生（DT）通过决策机制对其孪生目标（TT）施加改变并优化其属性。传统上，家庭自动化等领域采用基于规则的决策模型，以命令式方式根据期望的TT状态定义DT动作。然而随着用户规格不断演化，规则模型在开发与维护上愈发复杂，尤其在受天气或动态能源定价等动态变化系统影响的优化场景中尤为明显。因此作者希望寻找一种能应对动态环境、降低规则维护负担的替代决策范式。

Method: 提出无规则数字孪生（RDTs）：类似模型预测控制（MPC）这一成熟的无规则方法，RDT在纯声明式用户规格下自动产生最优决策。其实现依赖四个组件的组合——语义知识库、逻辑推理、仿真模型以及自主计算架构，且每个组件均基于广泛使用的标准或技术。

Result: 通过孵育器案例研究，并在虚拟办公室房间环境中针对基于规则的bang-bang控制器开展实验验证。结果表明：在维持期望室温以及最小化动态实时（spot）定价下的能源成本方面，RDT功能的表现优于该控制器。此外，由于采用组合式决策树构建，RDT在较长预测时域下需要可观的算力；其对特定问题域的适用性及较短时域的使用最终取决于用户。

Conclusion: RDTs为数字孪生提供了一种声明式、无规则的决策替代方案，在动态定价与温度控制场景中优于传统规则控制器，具备实证可行性。但其算力开销随预测时域增长而显著上升，实际应用需权衡问题域特性与预测时域长度，由用户自行决策。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) can be thought of as digital counterparts of physical objects, or more generally, twinning targets (TTs). To enact changes on and optimize for properties of their TTs, DTs use an element of decision-making. Traditionally, many domains, such as home automation, utilize rule-based decision-making models to imperatively define DT actions based on desired TT conditions. With evolving user specifications, rule-based models have become increasingly complex to develop and maintain, particularly in scenarios involving optimizations under dynamically changing systems, such as those affected by weather or dynamic energy pricing. We present an alternative in the form of ruleless digital twins (RDTs) that automatically produce optimal decisions with respect to purely declarative user specifications, similarly to the well-established ruleless approach of model predictive control. They do so through a combination of a semantic knowledge base, logical inference, simulation models, and an autonomic computing architecture, each exclusively based on a widely-used standard or technology. We evaluate our proof of concept through an incubator case study and experiments against a rule-based bang-bang controller in the context of a virtual office room environment. Results show core that RDT functionality performs better than the control in terms of maintaining desired room temperatures and minimizing energy costs under dynamically changing spot pricing. Additionally, due to combinatorial decision tree construction, RDTs require considerable computational power for longer prediction horizons, although their applicability to specific problem domains (and use of shorter horizons) ultimately falls on their users.

</details>


### [311] [Has LLM Screening Performance Stalled in Software Engineering Systematic Reviews?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10633)
*Aleksi Huotala,Miikka Kuutila,Mika Mäntylä*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文利用既有软件工程系统性综述筛选基准（SESR-Eval）及新构建的轻量数据集（SESR-Eval-Mini），评估了8个新大语言模型的文献筛选性能，并考察提示设计、筛选决策一致性及纳入/排除标准细化对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性综述的筛选环节依赖人工、耗时巨大；已有研究虽探索用大语言模型自动化该步骤，但模型迭代迅速，早期性能结论可能已过时，因此需要对新模型重新评估，并探索降低评估成本及优化筛选策略的途径。

Method: 以SESR-Eval软件工程SR筛选基准为数据基础，并采用统计功效抽样构建更小、成本更低的新数据集SESR-Eval-Mini；据此评估8个新LLM的筛选性能，比较新老模型差异；测试不同提示（prompt）；分析模型在筛选决策与标准层面的—致性（Gwet's AC1）；并检验细化纳入/排除标准对筛选性能的影响。

Result: 8个新LLM仅略优于7个旧模型，次级研究平均MCC由0.347升至0.365；次级研究间差异仍大于模型间差异；由标准级决策汇总整体筛选决策仅轻微降低性能；模型间筛选决策总体一致（平均Gwet's AC1=0.830），但部分纳入/排除标准分歧较大；细化标准略微提升召回率并使部分模型决策更易，但总体影响温和。

Conclusion: LLM尚不足以替代人工进行论文筛选，更新、更昂贵模型带来的优势非常有限；未来可探索基于智能体的方法、提示工程与进一步的标准细化三条研究路径。

Abstract: Screening in systematic reviews (SRs) is manual and time-consuming. Prior work has explored large language models (LLMs) for automating this step, but LLMs are evolving rapidly, so earlier performance claims may no longer accurately reflect their screening performance. We used an existing software engineering SR screening benchmark (SESR-Eval) as our data. We also power-sampled a new, smaller dataset (SESR-Eval-Mini) that allows evaluation at lower costs. Using this data, we evaluated eight new LLMs for screening performance. Additionally, we tested different prompts, analyzed LLM agreement in screening decisions and criteria, and examined the effect of refining the inclusion and exclusion criteria on screening performance. The eight new LLMs performed marginally better than the seven old ones: avg. MCC across secondary studies rose from 0.347 to 0.365. Differences between secondary studies are still bigger than between LLMs. Computing the overall screening decision from criterion-level decisions degraded screening performance only slightly. LLMs generally agree with each other in their corresponding screening decisions (mean Gwet's AC1 = 0.830), though certain inclusion and exclusion criteria showed larger disagreement than others. Refining the inclusion and exclusion criteria slightly improved recall and made decisions easier for some LLMs, but overall impacts of criteria refinement were modest. LLMs are not yet ready to replace humans in paper screening and the advantages new, more costly models bring, appear to be very limited. Agent-based approaches, prompt engineering, and further criteria refinement are three potential future research avenues.

</details>


### [312] [Can LLMs Simulate Novice Programmers' Misconceptions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10656)
*Batuhan Yeltekin,Daniel Bauer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文评估了13个LLM在代码追踪问题中生成、解决、模拟和诊断初学者编程迷思概念的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM能否胜任涉及初学者编程迷思概念的多项教育相关任务，并识别不同规模与类型模型的能力差异。

Method: 在代码追踪问题场景下，对13个LLM开展四项任务评测：迷思概念的生成、解决、模拟与诊断。

Result: 前沿模型能可靠完成各项任务；小模型（≤14B）在动态执行上表现吃力；经代码微调的模型在迷思概念模拟时会回退到正确执行；迷思概念诊断在True/False格式下显著易于开放式生成任务。

Conclusion: LLM在初学者编程迷思概念相关任务上的能力受模型规模、微调类型及任务格式显著影响，动态执行与开放式诊断仍是薄弱环节。

Abstract: We evaluate 13 LLMs on generating, solving, simulating, and diagnosing novice programming misconceptions on code-tracing problems. While frontier models reliably perform these tasks, small models ($\le$14B) struggle with dynamic execution. Notably, code-tuned models fail during misconception simulation by reverting to correct execution. Finally, misconception diagnosis is significantly easier in True/False formats than in open-ended generation tasks.

</details>


### [313] [Applying Security by Design at the Point of Execution: How Governed Security Requirements Affect the Security of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10659)
*Pedro Farinha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在生成代码时通过MCP服务器注入来自受治理的SbD-ToE知识库的安全需求，可显著提升生成代码的安全测试通过率与免于漏洞利用的比例，但联合"安全+功能"指标未显著改善，且现有基准只能检测预设弱点而非需求符合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "安全左移/安全设计"要求编码前定义安全需求，然而现行安全代码基准通常反其道而行：智能体在无需求条件下生成代码，再用未见过的安全测试评判，无法反映"需求驱动"场景。作者希望量化在执行时点提供受治理安全需求所带来的变化。

Method: 从受治理的安全设计知识库（SbD-ToE）中选取安全需求，经Model Context Protocol（MCP）服务器在生成时点交付给生成模型。在DualGauge（LLM裁判评分，59个Python任务）与BaxBench（容器内运行功能测试与真实漏洞利用，28个后端场景）两基准上开展实验，每任务单模型单次生成，并与作者提出的Oracle Security Reminder（85%）作对照；对功能失败进行逐例事后归因。

Result: DualGauge：通过全部安全测试的任务占比由44.1%升至78.0%，通过的安全测试占比由77.4%升至93.0%。BaxBench：在功能正确的解法中，无成功漏洞利用的比例由65%升至86%，与该文作者称为"不现实上界"的Oracle Security Reminder（85%）相当，且无需知晓测试内容。事后指定的"安全且功能正确"联合指标在两基准上均无显著提升。

Conclusion: 在执行时点交付受治理的安全需求似能提升生成代码的安全性。带治理需求的代码更严格，会在固化未在任务中给出的取值处导致功能测试失败（已逐例归因）。实验仅单模型、单次生成，需求源自作者开源手册且未评估其完备性。现有基准只能判断代码是否呈现评估器设计要检测的弱点，无法判断其是否满足适用安全需求，故建议改为度量"符合适用需求"的一致性。

Abstract: Security by design asks that security requirements are defined before code is written. Secure-code benchmarks typically measure the opposite situation: the agent receives the task without requirements and is scored with security tests it has not seen. We measured what changes when security requirements, selected from a governed security-by-design knowledge base (SbD-ToE) and delivered through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server, are given to the generator at the point of execution. On DualGauge, which scores with a language-model judge, we ran 59 Python tasks: the share of tasks passing all security tests rose from 44.1% to 78.0% and the share of security tests passed from 77.4% to 93.0%. On BaxBench, which runs functional tests and real exploits in containers, we ran 28 backend scenarios: among functionally correct solutions, the share with no successful exploit rose from 65% to 86%, comparable to the authors' Oracle Security Reminder (85%), which they call an unrealistic upper bound; the requirement delivery reached it without knowing the tests. The study has limits. The pre-specified joint secure-and-functional metric did not improve significantly on either benchmark. The code written with the governed requirements is stricter, and it failed functional tests that fix values the task never states; we attribute those failures case by case in a post-hoc analysis. Each experiment used one model and one generation per task. The requirements come from the author's open-source manual; we do not assess its completeness. Governed security requirements delivered at the point of execution appear to improve the security of generated code. Current benchmarks cannot tell whether that code meets the security requirements that apply to it, only whether it shows the weaknesses their evaluators were built to detect. We propose measuring conformance with the applicable requirements.

</details>


### [314] [Cross-Provider Review as a Runtime Contract for Coding Agents: A Controlled Pilot and Fault-Injection Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10961)
*Bowen Xu,Boyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"跨供应商咨询式评审契约"(独立资源池、受限权限、完整输入传递、可用语义输出、显式失败状态、可审计证据),并通过20轮配对试验、边界条件扫描与CLI实测验证其实现,期间发现并修复三个缺陷;正式测量队列因退出码未记录而失效,已重置为零并重新开始采集,未报告门控结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体常在同一工作站上协作却各自消耗不同的供应商与订阅额度;让第二个智能体复核已完成答案会额外消耗资源池,且可能毫无实质发现。因此需要一种既能约束成本、又能保证评审有效性与可审计性的跨供应商评审机制。

Method: 设计咨询式跨供应商评审契约:资源池隔离、有界执行、评审者权限受限、输入完整交付、语义输出可用、显式失败状态与逐次尝试的持久化证据。开展20轮配对开发回合的受控试点(由智能体撰写),进行跨两种评审后端的边界条件扫描,并执行真实CLI探测以检验只读约束。

Result: 20轮中有8轮出现实质性评审发现(95%精确区间19.1%–63.9%)。边界扫描复现了部分输入下的"假成功":四个截断层级历史通过、修复后失败;另发现并修复了进程回收期间的取消问题。CLI探测中Claude无写入工具,Codex在5/5只读试验中尝试写入且写入工具均失败,可弃置仓库未被改动。预注册影子研究累积25条正式观测后,精确运行期回归暴露第三处缺陷:评审者非零退出但给出结构良好裁决却被计为完成;因未逐次记录退出状态,暴露量无法回溯,25条正式记录与2条待定记录转为审计队列,测量有效队列归零重启。

Conclusion: 测试仅覆盖指定路径与版本,不代表实际部署可靠性。由于退出状态未被逐次记录导致测量有效性受损,当前无门控结果可报告;测量队列已重置并重新开始采集。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly share a workstation while drawing on separate providers and subscription allowances. A second agent can inspect a completed answer, but the call spends another pool and may provide no substantive finding. We describe an advisory cross-provider review contract: distinct resource pools, bounded execution, restricted reviewer capabilities, complete input delivery, usable semantic output, explicit failure states and durable per-attempt evidence. In a controlled, agent-authored pilot of 20 paired development turns, eight had a material reviewer finding (95% exact interval 19.1-63.9%). A boundary-condition scan across both reviewer backends reproduced a previously discovered false success on partial input: four truncation levels passed historically and failed after repair. The scan also found and repaired cancellation during process reaping. In real CLI probes, Claude had no writing tools; Codex attempted writes in five of five read-only trials, each write tool failed, and no disposable repository changed. These tests cover specified paths and versions, not field reliability. A preregistered shadow study of metadata-only review allocation accrued 25 formal observations before an exact-runtime regression found a third defect: a reviewer exiting nonzero with a well-formed verdict was counted as complete. Exit status was not recorded per attempt, so exposure cannot be resolved retrospectively. The 25 formal and two pending records remain an audit cohort; the measurement-valid cohort restarted at zero and collection has begun. No gate result is reported.

</details>


### [315] [Probabilistic Sensing, Deterministic Authority: Admitting Model-Produced Observations into Sufficiency-Checked Governance Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10978)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出将作者权限合约中仅存在于非结构化证据里的字段交由模型感知，感知输出仅作为带分数的观察记录，经声明误报上限拟合的准入策略映射为真/假/未知（未知即拒绝），再由确定性且经充分性校验的合约裁决；感知改变裁决的概率由合约各感知字段的错纳率与未知率之和上界约束，构成按合约索引的并集界推理，并以最小化该估计界作为充分合约选择的成本模型。在两个构造域、两族传感器（36,000次模型调用）的注册研究中无一格反驳该界；首次出现由感知导致的“拒绝转允许”翻转（21,000条测试裁决中13条，全部源自3条矛盾记录），且各翻转均落在注册界之下；感知感知式选择在4/4注册测试中选中低暴露合约；两族传感器分数有信息量但未校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权威合约所需字段常仅存在于非结构化证据中，需要模型感知；但模型输出不可直接作为合约输入（不可靠、未校准），因此需要一种可量化风险、可审计的准入与裁决框架，并给出感知对最终裁决影响的可界定上界，以支持在多个充分合约间做有原则的选择。

Method: 将模型输出限定为带分数的观察记录；用准入策略（阈值在留出集上拟合、以声明的误报上限为约束）把分数映射为真/假/未知，未知即拒绝；由确定性、经充分性校验的合约决定裁决。理论上以并集界给出“感知改变裁决”的概率上界——对合约各感知字段的错纳率与未知率求和，该界按合约索引；将最小化估计界作为充分合约选择的成本模型。实证上在两个构造域、两族传感器上开展注册研究，共36,000次模型调用，逐格检验该界。

Result: 36,000次模型调用的注册研究中，无一格反驳该界。首次在本研究计划中观察到由感知引起的“拒绝转允许”翻转：21,000条测试裁决中有13条，全部来自3条矛盾记录；在每个设有注册界的格中，各翻转均低于该界。感知感知式选择在4/4注册测试中选中了较低暴露的合约。两族传感器的分数均具信息量但未校准。

Conclusion: 感知仅以带分数观察记录进入流程、未知即拒绝并辅以并集界，可为合约裁决提供可量化的风险约束；最小化该界是选择充分合约的有效成本模型。结论的正确性相对于所声明的损失模型、候选表示与可达状态，所有域均为构造域，外推需谨慎。

Abstract: When a field that an authority contract needs exists only in unstructured evidence, a model can sense it. We admit the model's output only as an observation record with a score. An admission policy, with thresholds fitted on a held-out split at a declared false-positive ceiling, maps each score to true, false or unknown. Unknown denies. A deterministic, sufficiency-checked contract decides. The probability that sensing changes the verdict is bounded by the sum, over the contract's sensed fields, of the admitted-wrong and unknown rates. This is an instantiation of union-bound reasoning, indexed by the contract. Minimising the estimated bound is a valid cost model for choosing among sufficient contracts. In a registered study on two constructed domains with two sensor families (36,000 model calls), no cell refuted the bound. Deny-to-allow changes from sensing appeared for the first time in this programme: 13 of 21,000 test verdicts, all from 3 contradictory records; each flip in a cell with a registered bound lay under it. Sensing-aware selection picked the lower-exposure contract in 4 of 4 registered tests. Both sensors' scores were informative but not calibrated. Correctness is relative to the declared loss model, candidate representation and reachable states; all domains are constructed.

</details>


### [316] [Following Breadcrumbs in Code: What Accidentally Committed Ad-Hoc Logs Reveal about Developer Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11041)
*Yi-Hung Chou,Boyuan Jiang,Vidit Jain,Yiyang Min,April Yi Wang,James A. Jones*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过挖掘开发者误提交的临时日志（ad-hoc logs）及分析直播编程过程，构建了跨 Java、JavaScript、Python 的大规模数据集，揭示了临时日志的使用模式及其与代码复杂度的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者常插入临时 print/log 语句以理解运行时行为，但因这类日志具有短暂性（通常仅存在于本地、提交前被删除），难以捕获，导致相关系统性研究受限。

Method: 挖掘开发者误提交后又删除临时日志的"意外提交"（accidental commits），并结合直播编程（live-streamed programming）会话观察实际使用情况，据此构建跨 Java、JavaScript、Python 三种语言的大规模数据集。

Result: 发现跨语言的共性规律：临时日志倾向出现于运行时难以推理的程序区域；同时识别出语言特定模式，如 JavaScript 中常见于异步与回调函数、Java 中常见于线程相关类。此外，含临时日志的函数圈复杂度普遍高于总体函数群体，生产日志亦呈现类似关联。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于开发者运行时理解实践的实证洞见，并为支持调试与日志的工具研究者提供了有价值的数据集。

Abstract: Developers frequently insert temporary print or log statements, known as **ad-hoc logs**, to better understand program behavior at runtime, particularly when facing unexpected issues or complex control flows. Despite being a nearly universal practice, systematic study has been limited because these logs are ephemeral: they usually remain only in local environments and are removed before code is committed, making them difficult to capture. In this work, we addressed this challenge by mining accidental commits where developers unintentionally left ad-hoc logs and later deleted them, and by analyzing live-streamed programming sessions to observe their use in practice.
  Using these methods, we constructed a large dataset across three major programming languages (Java, JavaScript, and Python), enabling the large-scale investigation of logging practices.
  Our analysis reveals both common and language-specific patterns in where and how developers rely on ad-hoc logs. Across languages, ad-hoc logs tend to appear in program regions that are harder to reason about at runtime. We also identify distinctive language-level patterns, such as frequent use in asynchronous and callback functions in JavaScript and in thread-related classes in Java. In addition, functions containing ad-hoc logs generally have higher cyclomatic complexity than the overall function population. Production logs show a similar association, consistent with logging serving as a means of observing runtime behavior in structurally complex functions. Together, these findings provide empirical insight into developers' runtime comprehension practices and offer a valuable dataset for researchers and tool builders seeking to better support debugging and logging.

</details>


### [317] [IRONPROOF: COBOL-to-Python Transpilation with SMT-Based Equivalence Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11073)
*Dominik Blain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IRONPROOF 通过将 COBOL 与生成的 Python 编码为 Z3 公式，输出可机器校验的等价证书或反例，但证明范围限于"生成代码↔中间表示"，而非与原 COBOL 实现的等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL 向现代语言迁移（尤其 LLM 翻译）会改变程序实际计算内容且无等价性保证，因此需要机器可校验的等价性证据。

Method: 将 COBOL 解析为中间表示并生成 Python；把两者编码为共享输入上的 Z3 公式；若 UNSAT 则输出等价证书，若 SAT 则输出反例。辅以 PIC 界定溢出检测，并与 LLM 基线及 GnuCOBOL 运行时对照。

Result: 2,345 个 COBOL 文件中有 782 个进入检查路径：606 个（77.5%）证明等价，101 个部分验证，0 个被反证，75 个在流水线内失败并计入分母。独立编写程序通过率 52.6%（153/291），自写程序 92.3%。153 个独立证明中 37 个仅覆盖未建模输入的单次执行；606 个证明中仅 14 个对输出实际依赖的输入做了全称量化。两个公开业务语料（AWS CardDemo、IBM GenApp）均无法端到端建模。LLM-only 基线在 94 个独立程序中失败 54 个。溢出检测在可分析程序中标记 22.1% 越界输出（上界）。与 GnuCOBOL 3.2.0 对照时，49 个可执行独立证明中 24 个在证明域内某变量上与运行时不一致。

Conclusion: IRONPROOF 证明的是生成 Python 与中间表示（而非 COBOL 实现）之间的等价；证书覆盖窄、量化弱、真实业务程序难以端到端建模，与运行时存在实际分歧，因此该保证不能替代与 COBOL 实现的语义等价验证。

Abstract: Translating COBOL to a modern language can change what a program computes, and LLM translations carry no guarantee of equivalence. We present IRONPROOF, which parses COBOL into an intermediate representation, generates Python, encodes both as Z3 formulas over shared inputs, and emits either a machine-checkable equivalence certificate (UNSAT) or a counterexample (SAT). On 2,345 COBOL files (GnuCOBOL tests, NIST CCVS85, open-source collections, and programs we generated or wrote), 782 enter the checking path: 606 (77.5%) are proved equivalent, 101 are partially verified, none is refuted, and 75 fail inside our pipeline and stay in the denominator. On independently authored programs the rate is 52.6% (153 of 291), against 92.3% on programs we wrote. Of the 153 independent proofs, 37 cover a single execution of a program that reads input the encoder does not model, and across all 606 proofs only 14 quantify over an input that a proved output depends on. On two public business-application corpora (AWS CardDemo and IBM GenApp), the encoder models no program end to end. An April 2026 LLM-only baseline failed on 54 of 94 independently authored programs, counting translations our encoder could not verify. PIC-bounded overflow detection flags an out-of-range output in 22.1% of the programs it can analyze, an upper bound. An earlier confrontation with GnuCOBOL 3.2.0 found 24 of 49 executable independent proofs disagreeing with the runtime on a variable in the proof's domain. A proof establishes that the generated Python computes what our intermediate representation says the COBOL computes, not equivalence to a COBOL implementation.

</details>


### [318] [Skill Constellations: Tracing the Supply Chain of Agent Skills on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11169)
*Fahd Seddik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文构建了首个带日期的 agent skills 复制网络（覆盖 GitHub 上 2,193,119 次 skill 采用），揭示 skill 复制行为缺乏版本与溯源，少数仓库是几乎所有复制的源头且星标无法识别它们；作者用传播模型对仓库排序以供审计，最高排名的 100 个仓库可拦截 14.9% 的高风险 skill 后续采用，而星标最高的 100 个仅 0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码 agent（如 Claude Code、Codex）会以用户权限执行 SKILL.md 指令与脚本，开发者通过仓库间复制来分享 skill，形成一个没有注册表、版本和溯源的软件供应链。由此导致被复制 skill 的来源、安全修复的覆盖范围、以及哪些仓库需要审查均不明确。仅在单一时间点记录 skill 所在仓库的研究无法揭示谁复制了谁，因此需要时间维度的复制溯源分析。

Method: 基于 GitSkills 中每个 SKILL.md 的 git 历史，构建首个带日期的 agent skill 复制网络，覆盖 GitHub 上 2,193,119 次 skill 采用，并提供交互式查看器。进一步拟合“哪些仓库会被他人复制”的模型，用该模型对仓库进行审计优先级排序。

Result: 少数仓库是几乎所有复制的源头，且 GitHub 星标无法识别这些源头仓库；skill 副本几乎不随源头更新，源头修复很少传播到副本。按模型排序最高的 100 个仓库进行审查，可拦截 14.9% 的高风险 skill 后续采用，而星标最高的 100 个仓库仅 0.5%。

Conclusion: 该研究为安全工程师提供了一份在 skill 扩散前优先检查的短名单，并建议平台应分发带版本的引用（versioned references）而非直接分发副本，以改善 agent skill 供应链的溯源与安全修复可达性。

Abstract: Agent skills are SKILL.md instructions and scripts that AI coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex run with the permissions of their user. Developers share skills by copying them between repositories, which makes them a software supply chain without a registry, versions or provenance. The origin of a copied skill, the reach of a security fix and the repositories that warrant review are therefore unknown. Studies that record which repositories hold a skill at a single point in time cannot reveal who copied it from whom. We contribute the first dated copy network of agent skills, built from the git history of every SKILL.md in GitSkills and covering 2,193,119 skill adoptions across GitHub, together with an interactive viewer. A few repositories are the source of almost all copies, and GitHub stars do not identify them. Skill copies almost never change with their source, and a fix at the source therefore rarely reaches them. We fit a model of which repositories others copy from and use it to rank repositories for audit. Reviewing the 100 repositories it ranks highest prevents 14.9% of later adoptions of high-risk skills, against 0.5% for the 100 most starred, which gives security engineers a short list to check before a skill spreads. Platforms should therefore distribute versioned references rather than copies. Project Website: https://fahdseddik.github.io/Skill-Constellations/

</details>


### [319] [Who Pays the Review Cost? Triage, Fairness, and Accountability in AI-authored Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11179)
*Md Shamimur Rahman,Khairul Alam,Banani Roy,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对239名来自31个国家、具有代码评审经验的从业者进行混合方法问卷调查，考察AI作者身份在拉取请求评审中的作用。研究发现AI作者身份并非一刀切的拒绝信号，评审投入取决于AIPR是否具备可问责性、边界清晰的范围、基于项目的理由、CI之外的验证、贡献者响应性与可识别的合并后责任归属；并提出"可评审性债务"概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理正从本地代码辅助转向基于拉取请求的工作流，生成的贡献须在既有项目规范下被评审、解释与维护。已有研究开始刻画AI撰写的拉取请求（AIPRs），但对评审者如何管控其进入评审流程、AI作者身份如何重塑可信度与公平性、以及哪些准入机制能保护评审可持续性，仍认识不足。

Method: 混合方法问卷调查，覆盖239名来自31个国家、具备代码评审经验且对AIPR接触程度不同的从业者；通过情境设定（scenarios）与自我报告（self-reports）两种方式收集数据，并结合定性回答进行分析。

Result: AI作者身份并非分类式的拒绝信号；受访者将评审投入视为有条件的，取决于AIPR是否作为"可问责的贡献"进入，具体包括范围有界、理由扎根于项目、CI之外的验证、贡献者响应性以及可识别的合并后归属。该条件逻辑同样适用于新手的AIPR，受访者更强调其在当前评审过程中的可见参与，而非仅凭个人档案层面的声誉。定性回答进一步描述了当人类把关难以被观察时，指导、审查、推迟与路由等行为的变化。

Conclusion: 作者将缺失的理由、验证与归属概念化为"可评审性债务"（reviewability debt），即生成代码缺乏充分人类基础时评审者必须承担的工作。这些发现重构了AIPR治理的焦点：在生成贡献消耗稀缺的评审注意力之前，应先使人类判断变得可观察。

Abstract: AI coding agents are moving from local code assistance into pull-based workflows, where generated contributions must be reviewed, explained, and maintained within existing project norms. Although recent work has begun to characterize AI-authored pull requests (AIPRs), less is known about how reviewers govern their entry into review, how AI authorship reshapes credibility and fairness, and what intake mechanisms protect review sustainability. We report a mixed-method questionnaire survey of 239 practitioners from 31 countries with code-review experience and varying exposure to AIPRs. In the scenarios and self-reports elicited by the survey, AI authorship was not a categorical rejection signal. Instead, respondents described review effort as conditional on whether an AIPR arrived as an accountable contribution, with bounded scope, project-grounded rationale, validation beyond Continuous Integration (CI), contributor responsiveness, and identifiable post-merge ownership. This conditional logic extended to newcomer AIPRs, where respondents emphasized visible participation in the current review process over profile-level reputation alone. Qualitative responses further described shifts in mentoring, scrutiny, deferral, and routing when human stewardship was difficult to observe. We conceptualize missing rationale, validation, and ownership as reviewability debt, the work reviewers must absorb when generated code lacks sufficient human grounding. These findings reframe AIPR governance around making human judgment observable before generated contributions consume scarce reviewer attention.

</details>


### [320] [Characterizing Overconfident Failure in LLM-Based Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11300)
*Ravishka Rathnasuriya,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究代码大模型中的“过度自信”困境：错误程序常与正确程序具有相近的token级置信度。作者在4个开源代码模型和3个基于执行的基准上，考察不确定性度量能否作为执行正确性的可靠代理，并刻画全局与token级的过度自信现象，同时评估常见缓解策略。研究得到四点发现：现有不确定性信号仅提供部分且依赖模型的失败证据；程序级与token级过度自信持续存在；指令微调会提升失败生成的确定性却不改善正确性判别；常见缓解手段无法可靠解决该问题，而隐层表征可能隐含输出置信度未暴露的正确性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码可能语法合理但执行错误，而现有验证方法（测试、程序分析）不完整、昂贵或仅用于生成之后。模型自身的不确定性本应作为早期可靠性信号，但错误程序常带有与正确程序相当的token级置信度，即“过度自信”问题，因此需要系统研究该问题以判断不确定性信号是否可信。

Method: 在4个开源代码模型与3个执行基准上进行实证分析：首先检验现有不确定性度量能否作为代码生成执行正确性的代理；随后从全局程序级和局部token级刻画过度自信，比较置信度与熵摘要，并考察选择性生成与指令微调的影响；最后评估常见缓解策略能否减少该失败模式，并做探索性隐层表征分析。

Result: 四点发现：一，现有不确定性信号对执行失败只提供部分且依赖模型的证据；二，过度自信在程序级和token级均持续存在，基于不确定性的选择不能稳定提升被接受集合的准确率；三，指令微调会提高失败生成上的确定性，但不会持续改善正确性判别；四，常见缓解技术只改善可靠性的特定方面，不能可靠解决过度自信导致的失败。

Conclusion: 代码LLM的过度自信是持续且难以缓解的失败模式，输出置信度不足以可靠反映执行正确性；探索性潜空间分析提示隐层表征可能编码了输出置信度未暴露的正确性相关信息，为后续利用内部表征进行可靠性评估提供方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated code generation, but generated programs can appear syntactically plausible while still failing execution-based correctness checks. Existing validation methods, such as testing and program analysis, remain essential but are often incomplete, costly, or applied only after generation. Model-derived uncertainty is therefore a natural early reliability signal. This paper studies the dilemma of overconfidence in code LLMs where incorrect programs are often generated with token-level confidence comparable to correct programs. We study this dilemma across four open-source code models and three execution-based benchmarks. Our analysis begins by investigating whether existing uncertainty metrics provide reliable proxies for execution correctness in code generation. We then characterize overconfidence at both global and local token levels, asking whether incorrect programs remain indistinguishable from correct ones under confidence and entropy summaries, including selective generation and the limits of instruction tuning. Finally, we evaluate whether common mitigation strategies reduce this failure mode. Our study yields four findings. First, existing uncertainty signals provide only partial and model-dependent evidence of execution failure. Second, overconfidence persists at both program and token levels, and uncertainty-based selection does not consistently improve accepted-set accuracy. Third, instruction tuning can increase certainty on failing generations without consistently improving correctness discrimination. Fourth, common mitigation techniques improve specific aspects of reliability but do not reliably resolve overconfident failure. Our exploratory latent analysis suggests that hidden representations may encode correctness-related signals that output confidence does not expose.

</details>


### [321] [Closed-loop evaluation of LLM agents for embedded software development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11447)
*Jorge García-Carrasco,Sergio García-Carrasco,Alejandro Maté,Juan Trujillo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向嵌入式编码智能体的闭环评测基准，包含5个嵌入式控制任务与4种反馈场景，在模拟ESP32固件上评测7种GPT/Qwen配置共420次运行，结果显示gpt-5.4通过率最高但未饱和，本地模型能力正在提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正被广泛用作编码智能体，可进行文件编辑、构建测试、结果检查与迭代修复。嵌入式固件是苛刻的目标：其正确性取决于感知、时序与安全约束下的闭环行为，而非仅静态源码质量。然而现有嵌入式智能体评测有限，多侧重一次性合成或离线正确性，缺乏对闭环迭代能力的考察。

Method: 提出嵌入式编码智能体的闭环评测基准。每个任务提供纯文本工程描述、受限工作区以及可见的构建与运行时接口；智能体需将需求转化为实现与自验证步骤，并迭代直至达到所需设备行为。基准包含5个嵌入式控制任务与4种反馈场景：一次性生成、真实自验证、CI式红/绿反馈、oracle式详细反馈。实现面向模拟ESP32固件以保证可复现性。评测7种GPT族与Qwen族配置，每条件重复3次，共420次运行。

Result: gpt-5.4在受评配置中通过率最高，但未使基准饱和；qwen3.5-27B是观测到的最强本地模型；较小的本地模型在通过率与搜索效率上急剧下降。

Conclusion: 结果表明，具备能力的本地嵌入式编码智能体正在出现，同时该基准仍留有提升空间，可用于持续考察嵌入式场景下的闭环智能体能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as coding agents that edit files, run builds and tests, inspect execution results, and repair software iteratively. Embedded firmware is a demanding target because correctness depends on closed-loop behavior under sensing, timing, and safety constraints, not only on static source quality. Yet embedded-agent evaluation remains limited and often emphasizes one-shot synthesis or offline correctness.
  We present a benchmark for closed-loop evaluation of embedded coding agents. Each task provides a plain-text engineering description, constrained workspace, and visible build-and-runtime surface. The agent must translate requirements into implementation and self-verification steps, then iterate until the required device behavior is achieved. The suite contains five embedded-control tasks and four feedback scenarios: one-shot generation, realistic self-verification, CI-style red/green feedback, and oracle-style detailed feedback.
  The implementation targets simulated ESP32 firmware for reproducibility. We evaluate seven GPT-family and Qwen-family configurations across five tasks and four scenarios, with three repetitions per condition for 420 runs. gpt-5.4 has the highest pass rate among evaluated configurations but does not saturate the benchmark; qwen3.5-27B is the strongest observed local model; and smaller local models degrade sharply in pass rate and search efficiency. These results suggest that capable local embedded coding agents are emerging.

</details>


### [322] [Evaluating Local Language Model Agents for Reproducible Data Engineering: An Empirical Software Engineering Study of Mobility Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11482)
*Jorge García-Carrasco,Javier Sanchis,Alejandro Reina-Reina,Alejandro Maté,Juan Trujillo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出包含15项移动性工作流任务的基准，在消费级GPU上以一次性与闭环两种条件、五种重复评估十种本地开权重LLM配置，共1500次评分。结果表明闭环工作区使大于20亿参数模型的通过率提升26.7–52.0个百分点，最强配置达85.3%的产物级成功率，量化90亿参数模型以约6.5GB显存达69.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益充当软件与数据工程助手，但关于本地可部署开权重智能体的证据有限；已有评测多聚焦文本回复或孤立代码生成，忽视完整工程产物的有效性。作者希望检验本地智能体能否产出正确且可复现的数据工程产物，量化闭环工作区条件的作用，并考察模型规模、架构、量化、运行时、工具使用与失败之间的权衡。

Method: 构建覆盖数据发现、连接器、运输数据流处理、语义增强、特征工程、验证、可视化与报告共15项移动性工作流任务的基准；采用确定性检查器评估生成的脚本、表格、结构化文件、图表与报告。在消费级GPU上对十种本地配置分别在一性与闭环条件下评测，每个模型、模式与任务重复五次，共获得1500次评分尝试。

Result: 对于参数量大于20亿的模型，闭环工作区条件相较一次性生成将通过率提升26.7–52.0个百分点；最强配置达到85.3%的产物级成功率；量化后的90亿参数模型以约6.5GB显存占用达到69.3%。当中间产物能暴露智能体可检查并修复的错误时，增益最为显著。

Conclusion: 本地开权重智能体可支撑软件密集型数据工程工作中相当一部分任务，但其可靠性取决于模型能力、任务可验证性与确定性验证。该基准为在工程工作流中采用前评估完整智能体配置提供了可复现的方法。

Abstract: Context: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used as software and data-engineering assistants, yet evidence about locally deployable open-weight agents remains limited. Existing evaluations often emphasize textual responses or isolated code generation rather than the validity of complete engineering artifacts.
  Objectives: We evaluate whether local LLM agents can produce correct and reproducible data-engineering artifacts, quantify the effect of a closed-loop workspace condition, and examine trade-offs in model scale, architecture, quantization, runtime, tool use, and failure.
  Methods: We introduce a benchmark of fifteen mobility-workflow tasks covering data discovery, connectors, transport-feed processing, semantic enrichment, feature engineering, validation, visualization, and reporting. Deterministic checkers assess generated scripts, tables, structured files, figures, and reports. Ten local configurations are evaluated in one-shot and closed-loop conditions, with five repetitions per model, mode, and task, yielding 1,500 scored attempts on a consumer-grade GPU.
  Results: Among models larger than two billion parameters, the workspace condition increases pass rates by 26.7-52.0 percentage points over one-shot generation. The strongest configuration reaches 85.3% artifact-level success, and a quantized 9-billion-parameter model reaches 69.3% with an approximately 6.5 GB memory footprint. Gains are largest when intermediate artifacts expose errors the agent can inspect and repair.
  Conclusion: Local open-weight agents can support a meaningful subset of software-intensive data-engineering work, but reliability depends on model capability, task verifiability, and deterministic validation. The benchmark provides a reproducible method for evaluating complete agent configurations before adoption in engineering workflows.

</details>


### [323] [SSCBench: Evaluating the Evidential Validity of Fault-Injection Tests for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11514)
*Xincheng He,Wanli Dong,Zhaoqiang Guo,Yan Liu,Lei Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了工具使用型 LLM 智能体故障注入评估中的证据有效性问题，提出测量协议与 SSCBench 基准，揭示同一故障案例在不同执行中的证据条件差异，并主张在解释采用率前应报告执行实际实现的证据条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障注入被广泛用于评估工具使用型 LLM 智能体的可靠性，但现有研究忽视了关键问题：当智能体自身决定哪些权威观测在执行中可见时，故障采用的评估结果应如何解释。若不明确反证性观测能否在受影响事实首次使用前变得可见，则"采用即失败"的结论缺乏证据基础。

Method: 提出一种测量协议，用于界定：何种观测能反驳被注入的断言；这些观测能否在受影响事实首次使用前变得可见；以及被执行过程是否真正实现了该条件。基于该协议构建 SSCBench 实例，在两种 τ-bench 环境中、针对 4 种故障算子与 5 种智能体配置，评估了 1,191 次故障执行，并结合自动化轨迹分析。

Result: 实验表明：同一故障案例与智能体配置在不同执行中可实现显著不同的证据条件；即使支持"及时反证"主张的样本群体稀疏甚至缺失，聚合采用率仍可被定义。在 44 次最终出现反证的采用运行中，仅 17 次在首次使用前获得反证，27 次在之后才获得。此外，首次错误时机与后续立场修正并不一致；自动化轨迹分析能恢复采用判定，但难以可靠恢复进行时序诊断所需的首次错误依赖事件。

Conclusion: 执行所实现的证据条件，以及支持特定主张解释的样本群体，本身即构成故障注入评估的组成部分；在将采用解释为"在使用前反证条件下失败"之前，应先报告这些信息。

Abstract: Fault injection is increasingly used to evaluate the reliability of tool-using LLM agents. However, there has been limited study of how fault-adoption results should be interpreted when the agent itself determines which authoritative observations become visible during execution. In this paper, we present a systematic study of this evidential validity problem in agent fault-injection evaluation. We develop a measurement protocol that specifies what observations can refute an injected assertion, determines whether they can become visible before the affected fact is first used, and records whether the evaluated execution actually realizes this condition. We construct SSCBench as an instantiation of the protocol and evaluate four fault operators and five agent configurations over 1,191 faulted executions in two $τ$-bench environments. Our experiments show that an admitted fault case and agent configuration can realize substantially different evidential conditions across executions, and that aggregate adoption can remain well defined even when the population supporting a timely-counterevidence claim is sparse or absent. For example, among 44 adopted runs in which counterevidence eventually became visible, only 17 received it before first use, while 27 received it afterward. We also find that first-error timing and later stance revision need not coincide, and that automated trajectory analysis can recover adoption without reliably recovering the first faulty-reliance event needed for temporal diagnosis. We argue that the evidential condition realized by an execution and the population supporting a claim-specific interpretation are part of fault-injection evaluation itself and should be reported before adoption is interpreted as failure under pre-use counterevidence.

</details>


### [324] [Chronos Enables Code Agents to Reason over Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11578)
*Xin Yin,Yiang Zhang,Zhiyuan Peng,Chao Ni,Zhe Cui,Xiaohua Xin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Chronos是一个测试时框架，将历史合并的PR蒸馏为结构化经验卡片，并通过类型化图（代码层、开发者意图、组织关系）连接，利用语义搜索与加权多跳扩展检索相关经验，用于指导LLM代码代理的补丁生成与选择，在多个基准上提升解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史合并的Pull Request记录了代码库的设计决策、兼容性约束与实现模式，其中与新任务相关的经验可能分散在不同描述侧重各异的变更中，如何有效利用这些关联历史信息是提升LLM代码代理能力的动因。

Method: Chronos将已合并PR蒸馏为结构化经验卡片，用类型化图（代码级、开发者意图、组织关系）连接；通过语义搜索确定入口卡片，加权多跳扩展检索相关联的变更以供选择性阅读；同一记忆同时指导候选生成与补丁选择——一个聚焦补丁的变更代理与一个验证策略代理各生成补丁，再由演化管家（evolution steward）参考历史进行选择。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Verified上，完整工作流在所有六个被评估的LLM骨干上都提升了SWE-Agent，平均解决率从69.2%升至72.9%，采用MiniMax M2.5达79.8%；同骨干下SWE-Bench Pro从48.3%升至51.7%，FEA-Bench Lite从41.0%升至43.5%。两种经验引导的单代理变体均优于基础代理。在100个任务、每任务检索10张卡片的人工评估中，图接地检索将有用卡片平均数量从1.24提升至2.87（对比扁平语义检索）。

Conclusion: PR关系对于检索有用的仓库经验具有价值，且所评估的工作流在补丁生成与选择阶段应用这些经验是有效的。

Abstract: Historical pull requests record the design decisions, compatibility constraints, and implementation patterns behind a codebase's current state. Experience relevant to a new task can span related changes whose descriptions emphasize different concerns. We introduce Chronos, a test-time framework that makes this connected history available to large language model (LLM)-based code agents. Chronos distills merged pull requests into structured experience cards and connects them through a typed graph of code-level, developer-intent, and organizational relations. Semantic search identifies entry cards, and weighted multi-hop expansion retrieves connected changes for selective reading. The same memory guides candidate generation and patch selection: a patch-focused change agent and a validation-strategy agent each develop a patch, and an evolution steward consults history to select between them. On SWE-Bench Verified, the full workflow improves SWE-Agent across all six evaluated LLM backbones, raising the mean resolution rate from 69.2% to 72.9% and reaching 79.8% with MiniMax M2.5. With the same backbone, it raises resolution rates from 48.3% to 51.7% on SWE-Bench Pro and from 41.0% to 43.5% on FEA-Bench Lite. Both experience-guided single-agent variants also outperform the base agent. In a human evaluation on 100 tasks with ten cards retrieved per task, graph-grounded retrieval increases the mean number of useful cards from 1.24 to 2.87 over flat semantic retrieval. These results demonstrate the value of PR relations for retrieving useful repository experience and of the evaluated workflows for applying that experience during patch generation and selection.

</details>


### [325] [Runnable Commit Untangling for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11593)
*Jinfeng Jiang,Dongsun Kim,Dayi Lin,Zhou Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出现有提交解纠缠研究的两大局限（未保持代码可运行、缺乏维护收益的直接证据），提出首个保持可运行的智能体式解纠缠方法 RucTangle 与首个量化解纠缠历史对智能体修复缺陷帮助的评估框架 TangleEval。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体产生的补丁庞大且混杂多种开发目的，难以审查与维护。提交解纠缠有望将大补丁拆分为可管理的提交，但现有研究存在两点不足：一是未考虑解纠缠后的提交具有顺序性且应保持代码可运行，实践中维护者难以接受导致代码无法运行的提交；二是仅通过解纠缠提交与开发者原始提交的句法比较声称有助于维护，却未直接展示所声称的维护收益。

Method: 提出两项贡献：(1) RucTangle——首个在解纠缠提交的同时保证每个提交后代码仍可运行的智能体式方法；(2) TangleEval——首个量化"可管理、解纠缠的提交历史"如何帮助编码智能体修复缺陷的评估框架。在131个智能体生成的补丁上将 RucTangle 与四种解纠缠方法对比，并收集453个引入回归（使原本通过的测试失败）的智能体补丁，用另外两个编码智能体修复回归。

Result: RucTangle 生成的所有历史均可运行，而基线方法产生 20.6%–37.4% 的不可运行提交历史。在回归修复任务中，用 RucTangle 生成的历史增强智能体上下文，使 pass@1 获得 5.2% 的绝对提升。此外通过分析智能体轨迹，揭示了智能体如何利用解纠缠提交来定位与修复缺陷。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在编码智能体时代采用成熟的软件工程实践具有价值，并拓展了未来研究议程：智能体如何主动利用软件历史做出更好的开发决策。

Abstract: Coding agents produce large, tangled patches that mix multiple development purposes, making the code hard to review and maintain. Commit untangling offers the promise of organizing such large patches into untangled, manageable commits. This paper emphasizes two important limitations in existing commit untangling studies. First, they do not consider that untangled commits are ordered and should leave the code runnable. In practice, maintainers are unlikely to accept commits that prevent the code from running. Second, existing studies claim that commit untangling helps software maintenance. However, they conduct syntactic comparisons between the untangled commits and developers' original commits without directly showing the claimed maintenance benefits. To address these gaps, this paper makes two novel contributions: (1) RucTangle, the first agentic method that untangles commits while keeping the code runnable after each commit; and (2) TangleEval, the first evaluation framework that quantifies how untangled, manageable commit histories help coding agents repair bugs. We compare RucTangle against four untangling methods on 131 agent-generated patches. All histories produced by RucTangle are runnable, while baselines produce 20.6%-37.4% unrunnable commit histories. We further collect 453 agent-generated patches that introduce regressions (i.e., causing previously passing tests to fail) and ask two other coding agents to repair regressions. Augmenting agent context with RucTangle-produced histories yields 5.2% absolute improvement in pass@1. We also analyze agent trajectories to learn how they use untangled commits to navigate and fix bugs. Our findings demonstrate the value of adopting established software engineering practices in the era of coding agents, which broaden the future research agenda: how can agents actively use software history to make better development decisions?

</details>


### [326] [PolyCodeEval: Benchmarking Multilingual Code Generation from Functions to Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11618)
*Bowen Yang,Jiajun Jiang,Luxue Yu,Yihao Wang,Fengjie Li,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出PolyCodeEval——一个覆盖5种语言、从函数到仓库的多语言多粒度代码生成基准，含2590个来自58个真实可执行仓库的任务，统一基于执行的评测协议；实验表明现有方法在多粒度和多语言上仍表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型迈向仓库级软件工程，现有代码生成基准在语言覆盖、任务粒度和评测协议上彼此割裂，难以进行系统性比较。因此需要一个统一的多语言、多粒度基准来全面评估代码生成能力。

Method: 构建PolyCodeEval基准：2590个代码生成任务，覆盖从函数到仓库的不同粒度，来源于5种编程语言、58个真实可执行开源仓库；所有任务在统一的基于执行的协议下评测，并针对不同生成目标定制集成流程。基于该基准评测前沿大模型、专用SOTA方法和通用编码智能体。

Result: 现有方法难以跨粒度和语言正确生成完整代码片段：所研究方法在函数、文件、仓库上的正确率最高分别仅为71.7%、76.7%、31.0%，且不同语言间差异显著。配对实验表明，同一文件中相关函数的实现上下文可提升函数生成的可执行正确率。方法排名随任务粒度和编程语言变化。

Conclusion: 多语言、多粒度评测对全面评估代码生成能力至关重要；当前方法在仓库级生成等方面仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: As large language models increasingly move toward repository-level software engineering, existing code-generation benchmarks remain fragmented across language coverage, task granularity, and evaluation protocols, impeding systematic comparison. To address this gap, we present PolyCodeEval, a unified multilingual and multi-granularity benchmark for code generation. It comprises 2,590 code generation tasks spanning functions to repositories, derived from 58 real, executable open-source repositories in five programming languages. All tasks are evaluated under a unified execution-based protocol with integration procedures tailored to their generation targets. Building on this benchmark, we evaluate frontier large language models, state-of-the-art specialized methods, and general coding agents. Our results show that existing approaches still struggle to correctly generate complete code fragments across granularities and languages. Specifically, the studied methods generate at most 71.7%, 76.7%, and 31.0% correct functions, files, and repositories, respectively, with performance varying widely across languages. Paired experiments further show that implementation context from related functions in the same file improves the executable correctness of function generation. Method rankings also vary across task granularities and programming languages, highlighting the importance of multilingual, multi-granularity evaluation for comprehensively assessing code generation capabilities.

</details>


### [327] [One Skill Too Many: How Co-Installed Skills Conflict in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11647)
*Chaoliang Yan,Zihao Xu,Yuekang Li,Shangzhi Xu,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Siqi Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对编码智能体中的"技能冲突"进行实证研究：当两个功能相似的技能被共安装时，模型仅凭名称和描述选择，常导致已安装技能失去核心功能，而任务仍能完成，故仅考核任务完成度的基准无法发现该问题。基于20,947个仓库快照挖掘822,109个候选相似技能对，经LLM判定3,754对后确认312对，在三个模型上运行6,368次（169,294次工具调用、542智能体小时），报告五项发现并给出防护建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体通过SKILL.md扩展"技能"，但技能来自团队、开发者、插件、复制集合等独立来源，安装环境中可能共存多个功能相同的技能。模型仅凭名称与描述二选一，冲突时已安装技能会丧失核心功能（如禁止操作git），而任务仍能通过，因此只检查任务完成的基准会漏掉此类隐性退化。作者希望首次系统刻画该现象。

Method: 从20,947个仓库快照中挖掘822,109个候选相似技能对，由LLM对3,754个分层样本进行判定，得到312个确认冲突对；在三个模型上运行这些确认对，共6,368次运行、169,294次工具调用、542智能体小时，统计冲突发生率、类型、替代行为、安装位置与列出顺序的影响，以及引入预工具钩子的干预效果。

Result: (1) 冲突易发技能普遍：近四分之一已安装技能与同功能技能共存，37%被判定技能位于复制集合中；(2) 冲突对以规范性技能为主，其次为能力性技能；(3) 在不降低任务完成度的情况下，相似技能夺走约五分之一运行，先打开相似技能的运行损失超过三分之一仅由已安装技能提供的独占核心功能；(4) 安装位置决定哪个技能运行，列出顺序影响甚微，仅0.9%的替代运行在最终回复中说明所用技能；(5) 冲突在首次技能读取时即被决定，几乎总在文件修改前，在该读取处设置预工具钩子可将独占核心功能保真度恢复到"先打开已安装技能"的水平。

Conclusion: 相似技能冲突是编码智能体部署中的普遍且隐蔽的问题，任务完成度无法反映核心功能退化。基准测试应额外考核"独占核心功能"，平台应保护首次技能读取环节并明确展示实际运行的技能。

Abstract: Coding agents are extended with agent skills, directories whose SKILL.md tells the model when and how to perform a task. Because skills come from independent sources (teams, developers, plugins, copied collections), an installed skill can be co-installed with a similar skill doing the same job, and the model picks between them by name and description alone. In a conflict, the installed skill loses core functions (e.g., a ban on touching git) because the similar skill runs instead or changes what it does. The task still passes, so benchmarks that check only task completion miss such cases. We present the first empirical study of such conflicts. From snapshots of 20,947 repositories, we mine 822,109 candidate similar-skill pairs, have an LLM judge a stratified sample of 3,754, and run 312 confirmed pairs on three models (6,368 runs, 169,294 tool calls, 542 agent-hours). We report five findings. (1) Conflict-prone skills are common: nearly one in four installed skills is co-installed with one that does the same job, and 37% of judged skills sit inside copied collections. (2) Most such pairs involve normative skills, then capability skills.
