(3) Without lowering task completion, a similar skill takes one in five runs from the installed skill, and runs that open the similar skill first lose over a third of the exclusive core functions that only the installed skill fulfills. (4) Install location decides which skill runs, listing order barely matters, and the final reply names the skill used in only 0.9% of substituted runs. (5) Conflicts are decided at the first skill read, almost always before any file is changed, and a pre-tool hook at that read restores fidelity on exclusive core functions to the level of runs that open the installed skill first. Benchmarks should thus score exclusive core functions, and platforms should guard the first read and show which skill ran.

</details>


### [328] [Implementing the Spec Growth Engine: Preventing Spec-Code Divergence, and Growing the Spec with Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11725)
*Hartwig Grabowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Spec Growth Engine 的实现：以规格图锚定 AI 辅助软件开发，分为确定性层（防规格-代码偏离）与智能体层（扩展规格图）两层，通过三个独立开关与两种项目基线方式形成 18 种运行模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助软件开发中规格与代码容易偏离，且人类判断权下放程度难以控制。作者希望以规格图作为锚点，把"验证与度量"（确定性）和"生成与扩展"（智能体）分开，从而在保证一致性的同时，可调节自动化程度。

Method: 系统分两层。第一层为确定性引擎（不调用模型）：验证规格图、与代码 import 图比对、依据记录的测试证据判定节点 verified 状态、按可破坏范围对变更分类，防止规格-代码偏离。第二层用智能体（意图作者、规划者、编码者，各由独立模型扮演）按轮次扩展规格图，每轮后由确定性规则决定是否继续。通过三个独立开关——草稿门（draft gate）、破坏性变更的授权（delegation）、运行模式（run mode）——以及两种项目基线（floor）设定方式，组合出 18 种运行方式。同时定义了智能体与人类间沟通的门、请求与豁免（waivers），覆盖从全手工到无监督运行、事后由人复核的运作谱系。

Result: 给出 18 种项目运行组合的完整描述，明确了各门、请求与豁免的通信机制，以及从完全人工到无监督运行的操作区间；确定性层为智能体层提供可测量、可验证的度量基础。

Conclusion: 设计核心主张：自主运行的价值取决于对其进行度量的确定性实例。即自动化程度越高，越依赖确定性的验证与度量层来保证规格-代码一致性与变更安全性。

Abstract: The Spec Growth Engine anchors AI-assisted software development in a graph of specifications that the code is coupled to. This paper describes its implementation, which serves two tasks and keeps them apart as two layers. The first layer prevents spec-code divergence: a deterministic engine validates the spec graph, compares it with the code's import graph, earns a node's verified status from recorded test evidence, and classifies every change by what it can break -- without calling a model. The second layer grows the spec with agents: an intent author, a planner and a coder, each played by its own model, extend the graph in rounds, and a deterministic rule decides after each round whether the run goes on. How much of the human's judgement is delegated is set by three independent switches -- a draft gate, a delegation for breaking changes, and the run mode -- which, with two ways of laying a project's floor, give eighteen ways to run a project. We describe each of them, the gates, requests and waivers through which agents and the human communicate, and the spectrum of operation from entirely manual work to an unsupervised run whose decisions the human reviews afterwards. Throughout, one claim holds the design together: an autonomous run is worth only as much as the deterministic instance that measures it.

</details>


### [329] [MAP4CS: A Multi-dimensional Data Pruning Framework for Efficient Code Retriever Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11727)
*Yuxuan Chen,Mingwei Liu,Guangsheng Ou,Zekai Zhang,Zike Li,Yanlin Wang,Pelin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MAP4CS提出一种多维度感知的数据剪枝框架，通过融合句法结构、语义多样性与分布表示筛选出仅占5%的高质量核心子集，用于代码检索器的RAG适配，性能可媲美甚至超越全量微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG已成为软件工程中增强LLM领域知识的重要手段，但将检索器适配到不断演化的代码仓库面临挑战：海量代码语料含噪声与冗余。全量微调计算开销大，且因低质量样本带来负迁移导致性能次优；而简单随机采样又无法保证数据代表性。

Method: 提出MAP4CS（Multi-dimensional Awareness Pruning for Code Search）自适应数据剪枝框架。其核心是综合句法结构、语义多样性和分布表示三个维度识别小型高质量核心子集，并辅以严格的规则过滤流水线完成进一步筛选。

Result: 在两个大规模数据集上的实验表明，MAP4CS仅使用5%训练数据即持续优于随机采样基线；性能可与全量数据集微调相当甚至更优，验证了以数据为中心的AI中"少即是多"假设。语言分析进一步揭示其自适应优化机制：对冗余语料自动充当去重器，对混乱语料充当去噪器，构建出词汇多样且信息密集的训练语料。

Conclusion: 数据质量而非数量是代码检索器适配的关键，MAP4CS通过多维度剪枝以极小数据量实现全量微调级效果，并具备去重与去噪的自适应能力，为RAG中检索器的数据高效适配提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a cornerstone in software engineering for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs) with domain-specific knowledge. However, adapting retrievers to evolving code repositories remains challenging due to the noise and redundancy inherent in massive code corpora. Standard fine-tuning on the full corpus is computationally expensive and often leads to sub-optimal performance due to negative transfer from low-quality samples. Conversely, simple random sampling fails to guarantee data representativeness.
  To address these challenges, we propose MAP4CS (Multi-dimensional Awareness Pruning for Code Search), an adaptive data pruning framework. MAP4CS identifies a small, high-quality core subset by integrating syntactic structure, semantic diversity, and distributional representation, followed by a rigorous rule-based filtering pipeline. Extensive experiments on two large-scale datasets demonstrate that MAP4CS consistently outperforms random sampling baselines using only 5% of the training data. Remarkably, it achieves performance comparable to, or even superior to, fine-tuning on the full dataset, validating the ''less is more'' hypothesis in data-centric AI. Furthermore, linguistic analysis reveals an adaptive optimization mechanism: MAP4CS automatically functions as a de-duplicator for redundant corpora and a denoiser for chaotic ones, constructing a training corpus that is both lexically diverse and information-dense.

</details>


### [330] [ObliVul: Alert-Conditioned Safety Obligation Modeling and Bidirectional Counterfactual Validation for Code Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11801)
*Heyang Tan,Chengxin Gao,Xin Wen,Jiaxin Li,Rui Cao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ObliVul：面向告警条件的安全义务建模与双向反事实验证框架，用于漏洞检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实软件开发中，漏洞检测的难点不在发现可疑代码，而在从静态分析产生的大量候选告警中识别真正值得关注者。现有学习型方法多在函数或行级别识别可疑模式，难以围绕单个告警抽取完整程序证据；大语言模型虽能从局部程序事实推断风险源、危险操作、保护条件与状态前置条件，但这些语义无法可靠对齐到具体程序节点、依赖关系与传播路径，因而不足以验证相应安全义务是否真正影响当前告警。

Method: 对每个候选告警，先从代码属性图（CPG）中抽取局部证据包（LEP），并用大语言模型恢复候选安全义务；随后将安全义务与程序节点、依赖边和路径范围对齐，构建局部安全义务图（LSOG）；最后通过VAFI聚合互补的已验证告警证据，同时抑制冗余或较弱证据，输出函数级漏洞预测。

Result: 实验结果表明，ObliVul能有效区分易受攻击版本与修复版本，并减少修复代码上持续存在的误报。消融研究进一步证实了各组件对安全义务恢复、风险响应的验证及函数级漏洞推断的必要性。

Conclusion: ObliVul通过告警条件化的安全义务建模与双向反事实验证，提升了漏洞检测中对候选告警的判别能力并降低误报。

Abstract: In real-world software development, the primary challenge in vulnerability detection is often not finding suspicious code, but identifying which alerts among the large number of candidate alerts produced by static analysis truly warrant attention. Existing learning-based methods mainly identify suspicious patterns at the function or line level, making it difficult to extract complete program evidence centered on an individual alert. Although large language models can infer risk sources, dangerous operations, protection conditions, and state preconditions from local program facts, such semantic information cannot be reliably aligned with specific program nodes, dependency relations, and propagation paths, and is therefore insufficient to verify whether the corresponding safety obligations truly affect the current alert. To address this problem, we propose ObliVul, an alert-conditioned safety obligation modeling and bidirectional counterfactual validation framework for vulnerability detection. For each candidate alert, ObliVul first extracts a Local Evidence Pack (LEP) from the Code Property Graph (CPG) and uses a large language model to recover candidate safety obligations. It then aligns the safety obligations with program nodes, dependency edges, and path scopes to construct a Local Safety Obligation Graph (LSOG). Finally, VAFI aggregates complementary verified alert evidence while suppressing redundant or weaker evidence to produce a function-level vulnerability prediction. Experimental results show that ObliVul effectively distinguishes vulnerable versions from fixed versions and reduces persistent false positives on fixed code. Ablation studies further confirm the necessity of each component for safety obligation recovery, risk response validation, and function-level vulnerability inference.

</details>


### [331] [On the Risks of using LLM-Generated Tests for Regression Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11835)
*Mohammadali Charoosaei,Cedric Richter,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究揭示LLM生成的回归测试可能将错误行为固化为预期行为，形成"强制错误测试"，在后续版本中持续存在并逃避开发者测试套件检测，构成新型技术债务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基于被测代码自动生成回归测试虽能自动化测试流程，但当代码本身存在缺陷时，生成的测试可能编码并保留错误行为。作者希望探究这种风险在真实项目演进中的实际影响。

Method: 对SciPy、Qiskit和pandas项目主分支合并的145个pull request应用LLM回归测试生成，区分"揭示错误测试"（断言正确实现行为）与"强制错误测试"（断言错误行为），并追踪这些测试在后续提交中的存续与检测情况。

Result: 8%-17%的生成测试为强制错误测试，仅2.4%-4.8%揭示错误；若干后续提交后83%-91%的强制错误测试仍相关且通过；将各PR缺陷合并后83%-92%仍被强制，而开发者编写的测试套件仅检测出14%-30%。

Conclusion: 若无人工验证，LLM生成的回归测试可能将错误行为编码为预期行为，使缺陷跨越软件版本持续存在并逃避开发者测试套件，成为LLM回归测试的一种新型技术债务。

Abstract: Software is under constant evolution: developers continuously add features, fix bugs, and refactor code, and any of these changes may break existing functionality. Regression testing guards against such effects by capturing expected behavior in test cases. LLM-based test generation aims to automate this process by generating regression tests directly from the code under test. This is beneficial when the implementation is correct, but problematic when the code contains faults: the generated tests may then encode and preserve incorrect behavior. To investigate this risk, we apply LLM-based regression test generation to pull requests merged into the main branch of software projects and study the impact of the generated tests on subsequent project evolution. We distinguish between fault-revealing tests, which assert correctly implemented behavior, and fault-enforcing tests, which assert faulty behavior. Across 145 pull requests from SciPy, Qiskit, and pandas, 8%-17% of the generated tests are fault-enforcing, while only 2.4%-4.8% reveal faults. Fault-enforcing tests persist over time: after several subsequent commits, 83%-91% of them are still relevant and pass. They also accumulate: when the faults of all pull requests are combined in one codebase, 83%-92% remain enforced at the end of the commit history, and the developer-written test suite detects only 14%-30% of them. Our results reveal a fundamental risk of LLM-generated regression tests: without manual validation, they may encode faulty behavior as expected behavior, allowing bugs to persist across software revisions and largely evade developer-maintained test suites. LLM-based regression testing can thus give rise to a new form of technical debt.

</details>


### [332] [Trajectory-Guided Fault Localization for Agent Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11858)
*Yu Ge,Linna Xie,Zhong Li,Yu Pei,Tian Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 SkillMorph，一种基于轨迹引导故障定位的智能体技能演化方法，将执行证据链接到技能具体内容后再生成修订，在多个基准及实际内核生成场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能为代码智能体提供可复用指导，但不完整或不合适的指导会损害任务执行。现有基于 LLM 从执行反馈生成修订的方法，难以将修订建立在明确的行为证据之上。

Method: SkillMorph 的核心思想是在生成修订前将执行证据与技能具体内容关联。它比较重复运行与跨任务中抽象轨迹的成功与失败证据，并结合演化循环之间的变化来识别可疑动作；随后利用这些可疑动作定位技能中的编辑位置并生成相应修订。

Result: 在 SWE-Skills-Bench 和 CannBot 上，SkillMorph 演化出的技能在试次级准确率和执行一致性上持续优于原始技能及四种现有技能演化方法。此外将其应用于 AI 算子开发团队的自动化内核生成，已接受 6 个技能修订的 PR。

Conclusion: 轨迹引导的故障定位能够有效衔接执行证据与技能内容，实现自动化的技能精炼，提升智能体任务执行的准确率与一致性，并具备实际落地价值。

Abstract: Agent skills provide reusable guidance for code agents, but incomplete or unsuitable guidance can impair task execution. To reduce the manual effort of skill refinement, recent approaches use LLMs to generate revisions from execution feedback. However, grounding these revisions in explicit behavioral evidence remains challenging. To address this gap, we propose SkillMorph, a skill-evolution approach based on trajectory-guided fault localization in agent skills. Its core idea is to link execution evidence to specific skill contents before generating revisions. Specifically, SkillMorph compares failure and success evidence in abstracted trajectories across repeated runs and tasks, incorporating changes between evolution loops to identify suspicious actions. It then uses these suspicious actions to localize edit sites in the skills and generate corresponding revisions. Experiments on SWE-Skills-Bench and CannBot show that the skills evolved by SkillMorph consistently achieve higher trial-level accuracy and execution consistency than the original skills and those from four existing skill-evolution methods. We have also applied SkillMorph to automated kernel generation with an AI operator-development team, which has accepted 6 skill-revision pull requests.

</details>


### [333] [Evaluating Exact Output and Checkpoint-State Prediction in Real Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11889)
*Xiaohong Chen,David Bucur,Chenglong Ma,Yi Zhang,Lingming Zhang,Sriram Vishwanath,Grigore Rosu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个从源代码与输入预测最终输出及检查点状态的基准测试，包含400个案例、371个Python与C++程序，在7种设置、4个模型家族下评估。结果显示启用推理的设置显著优于关闭推理的设置，但长轨迹与状态跟踪任务的准确率明显下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRUXEval式输出预测评测多关注短输出，难以暴露长轨迹执行与循环内部/循环后状态跟踪中的错误。作者希望构建更细粒度的基准，通过配对短轨迹/长轨迹输入以及循环内外的检查点，揭示被短输出分数掩盖的模型缺陷。

Method: 构建包含400个案例（来自371个Python和C++程序）的基准，扩展CRUXEval式输出预测：提供配对的短轨迹与长轨迹输入，并设置循环内部与循环后的检查点任务。在无工具、无代码执行的条件下，用4个模型家族的7种设置进行评测，共计划11,200次预测，实际获得11,151个可评分响应，并进行源聚类分析与调整后的Python模型分析。

Result: 启用推理的设置比关闭推理的设置高出33.1至55.2个百分点。最强设置得分：短轨迹最终输出93.0%，长轨迹最终输出77.0%，两个状态任务分别为65.5%和63.5%；将缺失响应计为错误时结果仍成立。2,397个同源匹配的Python比较中，改用长轨迹输入带来528次对转错和147次反转。源聚类分析保持该准确率差距；调整后的Python模型未发现累积状态负载与错误之间存在正向增量关联。

Conclusion: 该基准能暴露仅凭短输出分数所隐藏的错误，说明模型在长轨迹和状态跟踪任务上存在明显短板。但由于改变输入与检查点任务同时改变了多个因素，所观察到的差距不能单独归因于轨迹长度或内部状态跟踪机制。

Abstract: We present a benchmark for predicting final output and checkpoint state from source and input alone. It extends CRUXEval-style output prediction with paired shorter- and longer-trace inputs and checkpoints inside and after a loop. The benchmark contains 400 cases from 371 Python and C++ programs, evaluated under seven settings from four model families without tools or code execution. Of 11,200 planned predictions, 11,151 produced gradable responses. Reasoning-enabled settings outperform their off counterparts by 33.1 to 55.2 percentage points on completed responses. The strongest setting scores 93.0% on shorter-trace final output, 77.0% on longer-trace final output, and 65.5% and 63.5% on the two state tasks; these scores also hold when missing responses count as wrong. Across 2,397 matched Python comparisons with identical source, changing to the longer-trace input yields 528 correct-to-wrong changes and 147 reversals. Source-clustered analyses preserve this accuracy gap, while adjusted Python models give no evidence of a positive incremental association between cumulative state load and error. Changed inputs and checkpoint tasks alter several factors together, so the gaps do not isolate trace length or an internal state-tracking mechanism. The benchmark exposes errors hidden by short-output scores alone.

</details>


### [334] [Can LLMs Fix It Without Code? Toward Automated Verification of No-Code Bug Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11963)
*Utku Boran Torun,Veli Karakaya,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于执行的自动化流水线，用执行器智能体在真实浏览器环境中验证LLM生成的"无代码修复"能否解决报告的问题。对322个修复的评估显示，最佳执行器下仍有不到一半的修复有效，且评估结果高度依赖执行器的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无代码修复（如引导用户修改设置、升级版本、调整工作流）可解决无效缺陷报告，但人工验证其是否真正解决问题需耗费大量开发者时间。因此需要自动化、基于执行的验证手段，并考察LLM生成无代码修复的实际能力。

Method: 构建基于执行的自动化流水线：执行器智能体按自然语言指令在真实浏览器环境中应用每个修复，问题专用检查器判断缺陷是否仍存在。评估322个由前研究基准的12种配置生成的无代码修复，涵盖Faulty Configuration、Wrong Version、External System &amp; Dependency三类缺陷报告。使用三个执行器重复流水线：两个计算机使用智能体OpenCUA-72B与Claude Sonnet 5，以及多模态智能体LLM Meta Muse Glimmer。

Result: 仅17.6%的候选问题能完成配置并通过两项健全性门槛。在322个修复中，依执行器不同，14.6%至49.7%的修复解决了缺陷；最强配置Claude Opus 4.6在Vanilla流水线下，于Claude Sonnet 5执行时解决率达74.1%。仅更换执行器即使同一配置的解决率平均变动38.8%，三个执行器仅在46.9%的修复上给出一致判断。与人工执行相比，执行器在66.1%至88.1%的抽样修复上与人工共识一致。

Conclusion: 即便在最佳执行器下，LLM生成的无代码修复也只有不到一半能真正解决所报缺陷，因此这类修复在交付用户前必须经过验证。基于执行的验证可提供该保障，但测得的能力强烈依赖执行器，评估研究必须报告并控制这一因素。

Abstract: A no-code fix resolves an invalid bug report by directing the user to change a setting, update to a version where the problem is already fixed, or adjust their workflow. Manually verifying whether a proposed no-code fix resolves the reported bug takes considerable developer time. This study proposes an automated, execution-based pipeline for evaluating the capability of large language models (LLMs) to generate no-code fixes in a real browser environment. We evaluate 322 no-code fixes generated by the 12 configurations released with the benchmark of a previous study, covering bug reports categorized as Faulty Configuration, Wrong Version, or External System & Dependency. An executor agent applies each fix by following its natural-language instructions, and an issue-specific checker determines whether the reported bug persists. We repeat the pipeline with three executors: two Computer-Use Agents, OpenCUA-72B and Claude Sonnet 5, and one multimodal agentic LLM, Meta's Muse Glimmer. Only 17.6% of the candidate issues could be set up and passed both sanity gates. Across the 322 fixes, 14.6% to 49.7% resolved the bug depending on the executor, and the strongest configuration, Claude Opus 4.6 in the Vanilla pipeline, resolved up to 74.1% of its fixes under Claude Sonnet 5. Changing only the executor shifted a configuration's resolution rate by 38.8% on average, and the three executors reached the same verdict on only 46.9% of the fixes. Compared with human execution, the executors matched the human consensus for 66.1% to 88.1% of the sampled fixes. Even under the best executor, fewer than half of the LLM-generated no-code fixes resolve the reported bug, so such fixes need verification before they reach users. Execution-based verification can provide this, but the measured capability depends strongly on the executor, which evaluations must report and control.

</details>


### [335] [Neural Network Verification for Deep Joint Source-Channel Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11994)
*Thanh Le,Hai Duong,Takeshi Matsumura,ThanhVu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对DeepJSCC解码器的界传播验证框架，通过扩展PReLU线性松弛、改写转置卷积为受限上采样-卷积形式、将瑞利衰落建模为前置结构扰动，并结合Lipschitz正则化全局鲁棒性训练GloRo，实现了DeepJSCC模型的最紧认证界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepJSCC虽能端到端传输数据，但在对抗扰动和信道干扰下重建质量会急剧下降，且此前没有任何方法能形式化地界定这种退化程度，缺乏可证明的鲁棒性保证。

Method: 提出界传播验证框架以约束给定信道噪声区域内的最坏重建误差。针对现有DNN验证器不支持的三个组件：扩展最先进的线性松弛优化技术以处理PReLU；将转置卷积替换为受限的上采样-卷积形式；将瑞利衰落建模为直接前置到解码器的结构扰动以降低验证问题维度。同时实例化Lipschitz正则化全局鲁棒性训练GloRo。

Result: 在图像传输的DeepJSCC模型上，该训练与结构编码结合使认证中位界降低至多41%，在信道估计10度误差下认证的安全案例约为GloRo加区间编码的十倍（192对19）。基于软件无线电的OFDM空口验证证实证书在真实硬件上成立，实测链路最坏误差0.082小于认证界0.128。

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现了DeepJSCC模型的紧认证，兼顾全局鲁棒性提升与验证效率，并通过真机OFDM实验证明认证界在实际无线链路上有效且保守。

Abstract: Deep joint source-channel coding (DeepJSCC) transmits data end-to-end over wireless channels using a neural encoder-decoder, but reconstruction quality can degrade sharply under adversarial perturbations and channel disturbances; no method formally bounds this degradation for DeepJSCC. We present the first bound-propagation framework for verifying DeepJSCC's decoder, bounding worst-case reconstruction error over a given wireless channel's noise region. Current deep neural network (DNN) verifiers do not support three DeepJSCC decoder components: parametric rectified linear activations (PReLU), transposed convolutions, and Rayleigh fading. We extend state-of-the-art techniques for optimization of linear relaxation in DNN verification for PReLU, replace the transposed convolution with its restricted upsample-then-convolution form, and formulate Rayleigh fading as a structural perturbation prepended directly into the decoder, thereby reducing the dimensionality of the verification problem. We also instantiate Lipschitz-regularized global robustness training, denoted GloRo, improving global robustness and enabling tight certification of DeepJSCC models for the first time. On DeepJSCC model for image transmission, this global robustness training procedure combined with structural encoding lowers the median certified bound by up to 41% and certifies about ten times more safe cases (192 against 19) than GloRo with interval encoding at a 10-degree error in channel estimation. Over-the-air validation with an orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) implementation on software-defined radio devices confirm the certificate holds on real hardware, with a worst observed error on radio link at 0.082 against a certified bound of 0.128.

</details>


### [336] [Reliability Characterization for N-version Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12149)
*Shunsuke Nagao,Fumio Machida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对N版本目标检测（OD），提出两个新的可靠性度量Cov_OD与Cer_OD，可在不依赖投票策略的情况下由各单模型检测结果直接计算，用以刻画多检测结果间的多样性（错误覆盖）与一致性（正确预测确定性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N版本OD通过多模型或多帧输入实现结果多样化并聚合以降低检测错误，其可靠性取决于多个检测结果之间的多样性与一致性。但现有指标如mAP和Accuracy仅基于聚合后的最终结果定义，无法刻画多样性与一致性，因而无法评估N版本OD系统可能配置的可靠性，也无法有效指导系统设计。

Method: 提出两个专为N版本OD定义的可靠性度量：OD错误覆盖率（Cov_OD）和OD正确预测确定性（Cer_OD），二者可直接由各单模型/单输入的独立检测结果计算，不依赖投票策略。通过自动驾驶仿真器中车辆N版本OD的案例研究，对所提度量特性进行实证分析，并用于版本组合选择、投票策略选取和系统增量构建。

Result: 案例研究显示所提度量具有独特性质，可用于：1）选择具有互补错误特征的版本组合；2）根据多样性与一致性画像选择有效的投票策略；3）通过两版本两两分析指导N版本OD系统的增量构建。

Conclusion: 现有聚合后指标不足以刻画N版本OD的可靠性，Cov_OD与Cer_OD能捕捉多检测结果间的多样性与一致性，为设计和构建可靠的N版本OD应用提供有效指导，凸显了此类度量指标的重要性。

Abstract: N-version object detection (OD) is an approach to diversifying detection results using multiple models or input frames and reducing detection errors by aggregating individual results. Diversity and consistency across multiple detection results are critical information for characterizing the reliability of possible configurations of N-version OD systems. However, existing performance metrics such as mAP and Accuracy fail to capture these factors, as they are defined solely on the final outcome after aggregation. To overcome this limitation, we propose two reliability metrics particularly defined for N-version OD, namely coverage of errors in OD (Cov_OD) and certainty of accurate prediction in OD (Cer_OD), which can be computed from individual detection results without relying on voting strategies. We empirically demonstrate the unique features of the proposed metrics through a case study of N-version OD for a vehicle in an autonomous-driving simulator. We show that the proposed metrics can be used for 1) selecting version combinations with complementary error characteristics, 2) choosing an effective voting strategy based on diversity and consistency profiles, and 3) guiding incremental construction of N-version OD systems via pairwise two-version analysis. These results highlight the importance of the metrics that can guide the design of reliable N-version OD applications.

</details>


### [337] [Cadence: Strategic Guidance for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12269)
*Minxing Wang,He Ye,Earl T. Barr,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出动态监控框架 Cadence，依据智能体实时执行健康状况自适应调度检查并分级给出指导，在 SWE-bench Lite 上显著提升解决率且保持令牌效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 编码智能体运行时监控器受限于静态指导触发机制：固定检查间隔会在严重错误时错过及时指导、在健康执行时产生额外开销；刚性启发式规则难以检测复杂推理错误。

Method: Cadence 包含两个核心模块：两级干预模块与检查调度器。干预模块对正常执行和小失误给出建议级（advisory）指导，对严重错误给出替换级（replacement）指导；调度器由干预级别驱动，在替换级指导后收紧监督（提高检查频率），在建议级指导后放松监督。

Result: 在 mini-swe-agent 与 Moatless 两个智能体、300 个 SWE-bench Lite 任务上评估，Cadence 在所有对比监控器中取得最高解决率：相较原始智能体在 mini-swe-agent 上提升 25.33%（+76 个解决任务），在 Moatless 上提升 15.67%（+47 个解决任务），同时令牌效率与最先进基线相当。

Conclusion: 基于执行健康状况的动态检查调度与分级指导，能够有效克服静态触发方案的局限，在提升 LLM 编码智能体可靠性与任务解决率的同时维持良好的效率。

Abstract: Runtime monitors are increasingly used to improve the reliability of LLM-based coding agents by inspecting execution trajectories and delivering corrective guidance upon detecting misbehavior. However, their effectiveness remains limited by static guidance triggering schemes. Existing monitors rely either on fixed inspection intervals, missing timely guidance during severe misbehaviors while incurring unnecessary overhead during healthy execution, or on rigid heuristic rules, failing to detect complex reasoning errors. To address these limitations, we propose Cadence, a dynamic monitoring framework that adaptively schedules inspections and delivers guidance according to the agent's real-time execution health. Cadence consists of two core modules: a two-tier intervention module and an inspection scheduler. The intervention module delivers advisory-level guidance for normal executions and minor lapses, while providing replacement-level guidance for severe misbehaviors. Driven by the intervention level, the scheduler adjusts the inspection frequency by tightening supervision after replacement-level guidance and relaxing it after advisory-level guidance. Evaluated on 300 SWE-bench Lite tasks across two distinct agents, mini-swe-agent and Moatless, Cadence achieves the highest resolve rate among all evaluated monitors. Specifically, Cadence outperforms vanilla agents by 25.33\% (+76 resolved tasks) on mini-swe-agent and 15.67\% (+47 resolved tasks) on Moatless, while maintaining competitive token efficiency compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [338] [TestPrism: Rethinking Test Evaluation Beyond a Single Reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12289)
*Han Li,Lingxiang Hu,Jiacheng Huang,Ziqian Jiang,Jingkai Luo,Wei Gao,Yunfan Tan,Zun Wang,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出测试生成新基准 TestPrism 与新方法 TestHelix：TestPrism 含 300 个测试任务、3000 个候选实现（有效/无效各半），其核心指标 Joint Success Function（JSF）要求生成的测试在初始程序状态失败、接受所有有效实现、拒绝所有无效实现；14 种基线智能体配置下 JSF 仅 28.00%，而单参考解成功率却达 59.67%，显示单参考评估高估了测试质量。TestHelix 通过异构测试-修复对合成、同行交叉验证与递归自我改进（RSI）提升 JSF 8.67–9.00 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试生成评估常以单一参考解为基准，忽视了多种合法实现的存在，从而高估测试质量、掩盖测试缺陷。

Method: 构建 TestPrism 基准（300 个来自 17 个来源的测试任务、3000 个候选实现，有效与无效各半），并提出 Joint Success Function 作为主指标（要求测试在初始程序状态失败、接受全部有效实现、拒绝全部无效实现）；在此基础上提出 TestHelix，结合测试-修复对的异构合成、同行交叉验证与递归自我改进（RSI）。

Result: 14 种基线编码智能体配置下 JSF 仅 28.00%，而单参考解成功率 59.67%；分析发现行为遗漏、不支持的断言及测试构造错误。TestHelix 在两个模型上将 JSF 较原生框架提升 8.67–9.00 个百分点。

Conclusion: 单参考解评估会显著高估测试质量；引入多候选实现与联合判定指标可更真实衡量测试有效性，而 TestHelix 能有效缓解现有测试生成的缺陷并提升 JSF。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents have advanced test generation across diverse programming tasks. However, the common practice of evaluating tests against a single reference solution overlooks alternative valid implementations and can overstate test quality. We introduce TestPrism, comprising 300 test tasks from 17 sources and 3000 candidate implementations, evenly split between valid and invalid solutions. Its primary metric, Joint Success Function, requires the generated tests to fail on the initial program state, accept every valid candidate, and reject every invalid candidate. Across fourteen baseline coding agent configurations, Joint Success Function reaches only 28.00%, whereas single reference success reaches 59.67%. Our analysis reveals missed behaviors, unsupported assertions, and faulty test construction. To address these weaknesses, we introduce TestHelix, which combines heterogeneous synthesis of test and repair pairs with peer cross validation and recursive self improvement (RSI). Across two models, TestHelix improves Joint Success Function by 8.67 to 9.00 percentage points over the native harness comparators in the TestHelix evaluation

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [339] [LIFT: A Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer for Unified Retrieval and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10556)
*Keji Miao,Enhao Cheng,Yan Li,Qun Li,Qi Zhang,Jie Yuan,Xiaoyong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LIFT模型，将交互分解为Request、Item、Context、Action有序状态并建模为因果序列，使召回与排序共享历史建模同时保留阶段特有信息，在两个数据集上取得最优联合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联推荐系统中召回与排序阶段使用相同的用户历史，但两阶段在同一交互过程的不同时点可获取的信息并不相同，导致信息使用与阶段需求不匹配。

Method: 提出生命周期感知的交互因子化Transformer（LIFT）：将每次交互分解为有序的Request、Item、Context、Action状态并建模为因果序列；召回读取Request状态，排序读取Context状态；采用Role-Conditioned Attention与轻量级Pre-LN Bias实例化该表示。

Result: 在ML-20M与淘宝数据集上，LIFT在所评估的联合模型中取得最高Joint Score，较最强基线分别提升4.9%与3.6%；损失权重扫描显示良好的召回—排序权衡，消融与规模分析进一步考察了生命周期序列构建、模型组件与容量设置。

Conclusion: LIFT通过生命周期状态分解统一召回与排序的历史建模，并保留阶段特有信息，在联合建模场景下优于强基线，且具备可调的召回—排序权衡与良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Cascaded recommender systems use the same user history for retrieval and ranking, while the two stages have access to different information at different points of an interaction. We propose the Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer (LIFT), which decomposes each interaction into ordered Request, Item, Context, and Action states and models them as a causal sequence. Retrieval reads the Request state, while ranking reads the Context state, allowing both tasks to share history modeling while preserving stage-specific information. LIFT instantiates this representation with Role-Conditioned Attention and a lightweight Pre-LN Bias. On ML-20M and Taobao, LIFT achieves the highest Joint Score among the evaluated joint models, improving over the strongest baselines by 4.9% and 3.6%, respectively. Loss-weight sweeps show favorable retrieval--ranking trade-offs, while ablations and scaling analyses further examine lifecycle sequence construction, model components, and capacity settings.

</details>


### [340] [Learning Multi-Step Query Rewriting via Corpus Feedback for Conversational Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10955)
*João Coelho,Hong Wang,Jie Yuan,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文将对话式查询改写（CQR）重构为序贯检索问题：智能体在类型化的改写操作空间（独立查询改写、词汇重写、伪文档合成及停止）中行动，借助检索证据迭代修正改写，仅用单一检索质量奖励经SFT+RL训练，在TopiOCQA与QReCC上优于检索对齐基线，并可零训练泛化至CAsT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CQR在检索前单步完成改写，改写结果在获得任何语料证据之前就已固定，无法修正指代消解或词汇选择错误。作者希望让改写过程与检索交替进行，使检索到的段落反过来指导后续改写，从而提升检索质量。

Method: 将CQR建模为序贯检索问题：智能体改写当前轮次→检索→基于返回段落决定下一步改写。动作空间为三个类型化改写操作（解析会话意图生成独立查询、生成词汇重写、合成伪文档以做文档到文档匹配）加一个终止动作。策略训练采用监督微调后接强化学习，仅使用单一检索质量奖励，不需人工改写标注。

Result: 在TopiOCQA与QReCC上，该智能体优于多种面向检索的基线；在不同检索后端上均保持有效性，并能零额外训练泛化到CAsT基准。分析表明，仅通过检索奖励优化，策略便自发学会将伪文档建立在早期步骤检索到的段落之上，显著提升检索效果。

Conclusion: 把CQR视为序贯检索问题，以检索质量为唯一奖励即可训练出有效的多步改写策略，且不依赖人工改写标注；检索证据可被智能体利用来逐步锚定改写，提升鲁棒性与跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Conversational Query Rewriting (CQR) turns a context dependent user turn into a standalone query for a retriever, and most methods do this in a single step from the dialogue history before retrieving once. The rewrite is therefore fixed before any corpus evidence is available to correct its reference resolution or its vocabulary. We recast CQR as a sequential retrieval problem: an agent rewrites the current turn, retrieves, and conditions its next rewrite on the returned passages. The agent acts in a typed space of three rewriting operations, resolving conversational intent into a standalone query, generating lexical reformulations, or synthesizing pseudo-documents for document-to-document matching, together with a stop action that ends the episode. We train the policy with supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning against a single retrieval-quality reward, using no human rewrite annotations. Across TopiOCQA and QReCC, the agent outperforms several retrieval-aligned baselines, while remaining effective across retrieval backends and generalizing to the CAsT benchmarks without additional training. Further analysis shows that, through retrieval-reward optimization alone, the learned policy develops a behavior of grounding pseudo-documents in passages retrieved by earlier steps, substantially improving retrieval.

</details>


### [341] [H2CE: Modeling Geo-Semantic Interactions for POI Reranking with Heterogeneous Two-Stage Cross-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11277)
*Zhengwei Bai,Moreno D'Incà,Danielle Class,Alessandro Moschitti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: H2CE 是一种异构两阶段交叉编码器，用于延迟受限的 POI 重排序。它通过桶化文本描述与精确标量双路表示数值特征，并以点式过滤加 Top-K 成对比较的架构融合语义、地理与质量信号，在 5,743 条本地搜索查询上取得 67.48% NDCG@5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POI 重排序需建模查询条件下的多目标权衡：词法语义、地理邻近性与评分/评论数等数值质量信号，同时满足实时服务约束。难点在于近距 POI 可能仅部分满足查询意图，而较远 POI 可能具备更强语义与质量证据，单一加权求和难以刻画这种非线性取舍。

Method: 提出 H2CE 异构两阶段交叉编码器。数值属性双路表示：桶化自然语言描述注入交叉编码器输入以支持语义-数值注意力，精确标量经专用 MLP 保留量级信息；语义与数值嵌入经隐空间聚合融合，实现超越标量加权的非线性交互。两阶段架构：Stage 1 对全部候选项点式打分以可扩展过滤，Stage 2 对 Top-K 候选做两两成对比较并用 Copeland 聚合，将成对开销从 O(N²) 降至 O(N+K(K-1))，并采用对齐训练。

Result: 在 5,743 条查询的本地搜索测试集上，H2CE 达到 67.48% NDCG@5，较 XGBoost LTR 绝对提升 +22.82%，较零样本 LLM 重排序器提升 +35.89%。成对阶段相较仅点式模型额外带来 +1.98% NDCG@5。消融实验验证了数值特征、隐空间聚合、Top-K 成对重排序与对齐训练的有效性。

Conclusion: H2CE 通过数值特征双路表示、隐空间聚合与延迟受限的两阶段点式-成对重排序，在实时约束下有效建模 POI 重排序中的语义、地理与质量多目标权衡，显著优于 LTR 与零样本 LLM 基线，且各组件均获消融支持。

Abstract: Point-of-Interest (POI) reranking in local search must model query-conditioned tradeoffs among lexical semantics, geospatial proximity, and numerical quality signals such as rating and review count, while remaining practical under real-time serving constraints. A close POI may only partially satisfy the query intent, while a farther one may offer stronger semantic and quality evidence. We present H2CE, a Heterogeneous Two-stage Cross-Encoder for latency-bounded POI reranking. H2CE represents numerical attributes in two complementary ways: bucketized natural-language descriptors are inserted into the cross-encoder input to support semantic--numeric attention, while exact scalar values are processed by dedicated MLPs to preserve magnitude information. The resulting semantic and numerical embeddings are fused through latent-space aggregation, enabling nonlinear interactions beyond scalar weighted sums. H2CE then applies a two-stage architecture: Stage 1 scores all candidates pointwise for scalable filtering, and Stage 2 performs head-to-head pairwise comparison among the top-$K$ candidates with Copeland aggregation, making fine-grained relative tradeoffs explicit while reducing pairwise cost from O(N^2) to O(N+K(K-1)). On a 5,743-query local search test set, H2CE achieves 67.48% NDCG@5, improving over XGBoost LTR by +22.82% absolute and over a zero-shot LLM reranker by +35.89%. The pairwise stage adds +1.98% NDCG@5 over the pointwise model alone. Ablations confirm the value of numerical features, latent aggregation, top-K pairwise reranking, and aligned training.

</details>


### [342] [EVIE: Evidence-Vector-Informed Embeddings for Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11553)
*Zifei Wang,Wei Wen,Qiang Ji,Qian-Wen Zhang,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EVIE 是一族原生视觉文档检索器，通过证据判定数据治理、双向师生学习与分层索引压缩三项创新，在精度与存储之间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索（VDR）方法难以兼顾细粒度页面理解与高效索引：基于 OCR 的文本检索增加预处理延迟并丢失视觉与结构线索；单向量视觉语言模型压缩整页为一个向量，匹配粒度不足；多向量 MaxSim 检索交互更细，但索引庞大且精度仍有提升空间。

Method: EVIE 集成三项创新：(1) 证据判定数据治理，用多模态判定器识别含答案的正样本并过滤不可靠负样本；(2) 双向师生学习，含对称 listwise 蒸馏与基于前缀的 Matryoshka 表示学习（Prefix-MRL），使单一学生检查点支持六个嵌套嵌入维度而无需重新编码；(3) 分层凝聚索引压缩（HAC），对页面 token 做带空间正则的聚类并存储语义质心，用于单阶段 MaxSim 检索。

Result: 在 ViDoRe V1、V2、V3 与 JinaVDR 共 138 个任务上验证：EVIE-8B 在 V3 上达 66.75 nDCG@10，超过最佳外部基线 1.43 点，四套件平均 79.51；EVIE-4.5B 配合 HAC 在每百万页仅 3.81 GiB 下保持 59.58 nDCG@10，向量负载降低 128 倍。

Conclusion: EVIE 通过在学习与索引全过程中保留查询相关的页面证据，改善了视觉文档检索的精度—存储权衡。

Abstract: Accurate and scalable visual document retrieval (VDR) requires both fine-grained page understanding and efficient indexing, yet existing approaches struggle to achieve both. OCR-based text retrieval adds preprocessing latency and can lose visual and structural cues needed to understand complex pages. Single-vector vision-language models bypass OCR, but compressing an entire page into one vector limits the granularity of query--document matching. Multi-vector retrievers with MaxSim provide finer interactions, yet demand large indexes and still leave room for accuracy improvements. We argue that overcoming these limitations requires preserving query-relevant page evidence throughout representation learning and index construction. To this end, we introduce \textbf{\textit{EVIE}} (Evidence-Vector-Informed Embeddings), a family of native visual document retrievers integrating three key innovations: (1) Evidence-judged data governance, which uses a multimodal judge to identify answer-bearing positives and filter unreliable negatives. (2) Bidirectional teacher--student learning with symmetric listwise distillation and prefix-based Matryoshka representation learning (Prefix-MRL), enabling one student checkpoint to serve six nested embedding dimensions without re-encoding. (3) Hierarchical agglomerative index compression (HAC), which clusters page tokens with spatial regularization and stores semantic centroids for single-stage MaxSim retrieval. Extensive experiments across 138 tasks from ViDoRe V1, V2, V3, and JinaVDR validate EVIE. EVIE-8B achieves 66.75 nDCG@10 on V3, exceeding the best external baseline by 1.43 points, with a four-suite average of 79.51. EVIE-4.5B with HAC retains 59.58 nDCG@10 at only 3.81 GiB per million pages, reducing vector payload by $128\times$. Together, these results improve the accuracy--storage trade-off for visual document retrieval.

</details>


### [343] [Overview of the NTCIR-19 Automatic Evaluation of LLMs 2 (AEOLLM-2) Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11598)
*Junjie Chen,Yuxi Dong,Haitao Li,Yiqun Liu,Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文是NTCIR-19 AEOLLM-2任务的概览论文，该任务在AEOLLM基础上新增“深度研究评估”子任务，聚焦LLM长文本生成的自动评估，共收到10支团队的91个提交结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）自动评估方法已有初步探索（NTCIR-18 AEOLLM），但在长文本生成场景下，尤其是LLM生成的长篇深度研究报告的质量自动评估仍缺乏系统研究。因此提出AEOLLM-2以进一步探究该问题。

Method: 在NTCIR-19组织中设立AEOLLM-2任务；新增Deep Research Evaluation子任务，要求参赛者开发自动评估方法对LLM生成的长篇深度研究报告质量进行打分，并以人工标注的ground-truth标签作为参照来度量各方法性能。论文同时介绍了任务背景、数据集构建、评估指标、参赛方法及最终结果。

Result: 共收到来自10支团队的91个runs（提交结果）。

Conclusion: AEOLLM-2通过引入深度研究评估子任务，推动了LLM长文本生成自动评估方法的研究，并为后续评测提供了数据集、指标与基线结果参考。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide an overview of the NTCIR-19 Automatic Evaluation of LLMs 2 (AEOLLM-2) task. Building on the success of the NTCIR-18 core task AEOLLM, we proposed AEOLLM-2 for NTCIR-19 to further investigate automatic evaluation methods for Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly in long-form text generation scenarios. In AEOLLM-2, we introduced a new subtask, Deep Research Evaluation, which focuses on the automatic evaluation of long-form deep research reports generated by LLMs. Participants developed evaluation methods to automatically assess the quality of these reports, and the performance of each method was measured by comparing its scores against human-annotated ground-truth labels. This year, we received 91 runs from 10 teams in total. This paper describes the background of the task, the dataset construction, the evaluation measures, the participants' methods, and the final evaluation results.

</details>


### [344] [SkillContrast: Difference-Guided Text Selection for Agent Skill Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11650)
*Jiandong Ding,Honglei Ji,Ming Liu,Tao Duan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SkillContrast：一种免训练的技能选择器，通过对比检索到的相似技能、保留其差异文本及局部上下文，为预训练重排器提供更紧凑的输入，从而在相同输入长度下优于TF-IDF查询选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似的智能体技能往往共享指令，仅在使用条件上存在差异。基于查询的文本选择容易保留共享指令而忽略这些条件差异，导致难以区分有用技能与其标记的风险性"兄弟"技能。

Method: 提出SkillContrast，一种免训练的选择器：对比检索到的技能，提取它们之间的差异文本并结合局部上下文，供预训练重排器使用。在SameCapRisk-Bench的1,235个请求上，与TF-IDF查询选择在相同单候选输入长度下比较，涵盖2种检索器和2种重排器规模；并通过长度匹配的组件替换进行消融分析。

Result: 相比TF-IDF查询选择，SkillContrast多产生54–72个干净命中（即检索到有用技能而未检索到其标记风险性兄弟技能）。长度匹配的组件替换表明差异文本是主要贡献因素，上下文效应较小且混杂。相比完整技能正文，SkillContrast减少51.1–58.8%的模型输入token；在0.6B模型上干净命中少10–18个，在4B模型上干净命中数持平或更高。

Conclusion: 候选相对差异可以补充查询相关性，用于选择紧凑的重排输入，在减少token消耗的同时保持或提升重排效果。

Abstract: Similar agent skills can share instructions but differ in their conditions of use. Query-based text selection may retain shared instructions and omit these distinctions. We introduce SkillContrast, a training-free selector that compares retrieved skills and retains their differing text with local context for a pretrained reranker. On 1,235 requests from SameCapRisk-Bench, it yields 54-72 more clean hits (requests that retrieve a helpful skill without its marked risky sibling) than TF-IDF query selection at identical per-candidate input lengths, across 2 retrievers and 2 reranker sizes. Length-matched component replacements identify differing text as the main contributor in the primary setting, with smaller, mixed context effects. Relative to full skill bodies, SkillContrast uses 51.1-58.8% fewer model-input tokens, with 10-18 fewer clean hits at 0.6B and matching or higher observed clean-hit counts at 4B. Candidate-relative differences thus complement query relevance in selecting compact reranking inputs.

</details>


### [345] [Autoregressive Retriever: Improving Query Understanding from Item Feedback for Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11666)
*Jianfei Zhao,Yifan Wang,Feng Zhang,Xin Sun,Chong Feng,Zhixing Tan,Yang Luo,Boyuan Pan,Xu Kai,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 AutoRegressive Retriever (ARR)，一种可交替检索与更新查询表示的多模态检索模型，通过逐步对比监督微调与强化学习优化反馈项选择，在域内与零样本基准上平均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通用多模态检索对查询只编码一次，并基于嵌入相似度独立排序索引项，虽然搜索高效，但即使检索到的条目有助于澄清信息需求，查询表示也保持不变，缺乏反馈利用能力。

Method: ARR 在检索一个条目与更新查询嵌入之间交替，并用最终嵌入对集合排序。方法包含两部分训练：监督微调通过逐步对比监督教会编码器利用反馈；强化学习将反馈项视为动作，用相关项最终倒数排名（reciprocal rank）优化其选择，并通过查询侧适配器在固定条目索引上实现该优化。

Result: ARR 在域内和零样本检索基准上均表现强劲，平均超过所比较基线。进一步分析表明，反馈在推理阶段提升检索效果；且带反馈训练也能改善初始查询嵌入，即在观察到任何条目之前就已受益。

Conclusion: ARR 验证了让查询表示在检索过程中借助反馈项自适应更新可提升多模态检索性能，并兼容固定索引，训练中引入反馈还能反哺初始查询编码质量。

Abstract: Universal multimodal retrieval typically encodes a query once and ranks independently indexed items by embedding similarity. This design supports efficient search, but leaves the query representation unchanged even when retrieved items could help clarify the information need. We introduce the AutoRegressive Retriever (ARR), a multimodal retrieval model that learns both to select informative items and to use their content to refine subsequent retrieval. ARR alternates between retrieving an item and updating the query embedding, then uses the final embedding to rank the collection. Supervised fine-tuning teaches the encoder to use feedback through stepwise contrastive supervision. Reinforcement learning treats feedback items as actions and optimizes their selection using the final reciprocal rank of a relevant item. A query-side adapter enables this optimization against a fixed item index. ARR demonstrates strong retrieval performance on both in-domain and zero-shot benchmarks, outperforming the compared baselines on average. Further analyses show that feedback improves retrieval at inference time and that training with feedback also improves the initial query embedding, before any item is observed.

</details>


### [346] [Chaos in the Text: Revealing the Modality Preference in Mixed-Modality Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11816)
*Yubo Sun,Chunyi Peng,Yukun Yan,Zhenghao Liu,Zhipeng Xu,Sen Mei,Linlin Xin,Zheni Zeng,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统考察了混合语料（文本、图像、图文融合文档）下的稠密检索器，发现性能对模态组成高度敏感，呈V型曲线，并提出Trident方法通过多正视图InfoNCE平衡模态偏好，提升混合模态检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索在纯文本和纯图像语料上进展显著，但其能力能否可靠迁移到含文本、图像及图文融合文档的混合语料尚不明确；且现有检索器在不同模态组成下表现差异大，存在模态偏好与文本干扰问题。

Method: 作者跨架构系统评估检索器性能对模态组成的敏感性；通过逐步用语义对应的文本替换图像文档来分析性能变化曲线；并发现文本表示获得系统性更高相似度分数（模态偏好）导致无关文本排名超过相关图像。为此提出Trident：为每个文档构造文本、图像、图文融合三种共等正视图，通过Multi-Positive View InfoNCE联合优化相关性判别与正视图平衡。

Result: 检索性能随模态组成变化呈显著V型曲线，单模态语料表现强，模态共存时显著下降；无关文本比等量无关图像造成更严重退化（称为Chaos in the Text）。在视觉文档与自然图像基准上，Trident在CLIP和VLM架构上均提升混合模态检索，降低对模态组成和文本干扰项的敏感性，并提高单模态平均检索性能。

Conclusion: 混合模态检索的关键瓶颈是模态组成敏感性与文本模态偏好，Trident通过构建共等正视图并采用多正视图InfoNCE优化，有效缓解文本干扰、平衡模态贡献，从而在多种架构与基准上实现更稳健的混合模态检索。

Abstract: Dense retrievers have made significant progress on text and image corpora, but whether these capabilities extend reliably to mixed corpora containing text, image, and fused text-image documents remains unclear. In this paper, we systematically examine retrievers across architectures and find that their performance is highly sensitive to modality composition. As image documents are progressively replaced with semantically corresponding text representations, retrieval performance follows a pronounced V-shaped curve, remaining strong on single-modality corpora but degrading substantially when modalities coexist. In particular, irrelevant text causes more severe degradation than an equal number of irrelevant images, a phenomenon we term Chaos in the Text. Further analysis reveals modality preference, whereby text representations receive systematically higher similarity scores, allowing irrelevant text to outrank relevant images. To mitigate this bias, we introduce Trident, which constructs text, image, and fused text-image views of each document as co-equal positives and jointly optimizes relevance discrimination and positive-view balance through Multi-Positive View InfoNCE. Experiments across visual document and natural image benchmarks show that trident improves mixed-modality retrieval on both CLIP-based and VLM-based architectures, reduces sensitivity to modality composition and text distractors, and increases average single-modality retrieval performance.

</details>


### [347] [Project Greenhouse: Progress Toward Fully Open and Sovereign Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11922)
*Jimmy Lin,Sahel Sharifymoghaddam,Lingwei Gu,Nour Jedidi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Project Greenhouse 探索用有限算力从零构建完全开放、自主可控的 agentic search 模型。首个里程碑是仅用常见公开数据集，通过"从零预训练 + 监督微调"两步配方，构建有竞争力的 pointwise decoder-only 重排序器，不依赖第三方开放权重骨干，全流程自主可控，且主要实验仅用少量 GPU 完成，并发布模型检查点与全部可复现产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多依赖第三方开放权重骨干模型，训练过程无法端到端掌控；作者希望验证以有限计算资源即可构建完全开放、自主可控（sovereign）的 agentic search 模型这一命题，并推动透明、可独立复现的研究范式。

Method: 两步配方：第一步从零（from scratch）预训练，第二步监督微调（SFT），仅使用常见公开数据集，不使用任何第三方开放权重骨干，从而实现对模型训练端到端的完全控制；整体实验在少量 GPU 上完成。

Result: 在有限算力条件下成功训练出具有竞争力的 pointwise decoder-only 重排序器，验证了不依赖第三方骨干、仅用公开数据即可达成该目标；同时发布 Gaggle 系列模型检查点，以及数据、代码和配置等产物，支持全流程的透明独立复现。

Conclusion: 该工作证明以少量 GPU 和公开数据从零构建竞争性重排序器是可行的，构成对更宏大命题（构建完全开放、自主的 agentic search 模型）的第一步验证，并为后续研究提供了可复现的基础与开放产物。

Abstract: Project Greenhouse represents our exploration of a simple thesis: We believe that it is possible to build fully open and sovereign models for agentic search with only modest computational resources. As a first milestone, we describe how to build a competitive pointwise decoder-only reranker using a simple two-step recipe comprising pre-training from scratch followed by supervised fine-tuning, starting only from commonly available datasets. Contrary to the dominant approach in the literature, we do not rely on existing open-weight backbones from third parties, and thus we are fully in control of model training, from end to end. We were able to accomplish the bulk of our experiments using no more than a handful of GPUs. This report articulates the importance and benefits of our approach, and we share artifacts that enable transparent, independent reproduction of all aspects of model training. Beyond data, code, and configurations that capture our efforts, we also release checkpoints for our family of Gaggle models, demonstrating the feasibility of our approach and providing a first step toward validating our broader thesis.

</details>


### [348] [NativeScope: Relation-Localized Retrieval over Native Topology with a Correct Anchor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12243)
*Long Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 NativeScope，一种面向已知锚点与关系的「先定域再排序」检索方法，将查询表示为 q -> (A, r, B)，先用锚点与关系选定原生单元，再用目标词排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索通常仅按文本块与问题的语义相似度排序，忽略了数据系统本身已有的结构信息（如章节归属、会话边界、原生顺序），导致检索范围过宽、精度受限。

Method: 将查询形式化为 q -> (A, r, B)：锚点 A 与关系 r 通过 belonging、before、after 等算子选定原生单元（scope），目标项 B 仅对与所选范围重叠的块进行排序；另设内部变体 NS-FullQ，用完整问题对同一候选集排序。在 QASPER 与 LongMemEval 派生的 200 条受控文档与记忆记录上，于 1,024 token 预算下评测两种方法。

Result: NativeScope 的原生单元召回率在文档上达 89.28%、记忆上达 72.50%，较实例级 Dense RAG 分别提升 42.75 与 22.00 个百分点；NS-FullQ 为 87.78% 与 68.50%，与 NativeScope 的差异不具结论性。使用自动 Top-1 锚点时，记忆召回率降至 35.50%。

Conclusion: 主要增益来自关系定域而非更短的排序查询；当锚点坐标与原生关系可靠时 NativeScope 有效，但硬定域会继承定位接口的错误。

Abstract: Dense retrieval usually ranks text chunks by their semantic similarity to a question. This ignores structure that many data systems already store, including section membership, session boundaries, and native order. We propose NativeScope, a scope-then-rank method for queries with a known anchor and relation. It represents a query as q -> (A, r, B). The anchor A and relation r select native units through belonging, before, or after operators, and the target term B ranks only chunks that overlap the selected scope. An internal variant, NS-FullQ, ranks the same candidates with the full question. We evaluate both methods on 200 controlled document and memory records derived from QASPER and LongMemEval under a 1,024-token budget. NativeScope attains native-unit recall of 89.28 percent for documents and 72.50 percent for memories, improving over instance-wide Dense RAG by 42.75 and 22.00 percentage points. NS-FullQ reaches 87.78 percent and 68.50 percent; its differences from NativeScope are inconclusive, locating the primary gain in relational scoping rather than the shorter ranking query. With automatic Top-1 anchors, memory recall falls to 35.50 percent. NativeScope is therefore effective when anchor coordinates and native relations are reliable, but hard scoping inherits errors from the localization interface.

</details>


### [349] [Compact and Efficient Indexes for Learned Sparse Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12300)
*Franco Maria Nardini,Luca Rizzo,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出在SEISMIC基础上重构倒排索引与正向索引，以减少学习型稀疏检索索引的内存占用并加速检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型稀疏检索索引性能强但内存占用大，需在不牺牲检索效率的前提下显著降低内存开销。

Method: 倒排索引用medoids替代逐块摘要，将块元数据压缩为单文档ID；正向索引重排词表、用DOTPACKING8编码Δ-gap并对值做4-bit量化，同时提出JUMPDOT分块点积内核；该正向压缩可独立集成，并已在KANNOLO中验证。

Result: 在MS MARCO上用三种先进学习稀疏编码器评测：同等精度下查询最高快5.3倍且内存约少3倍；最受限内存场景下最高快1.9倍且内存最高少3.9倍。

Conclusion: 通过倒排与正向索引协同压缩，可显著改善学习型稀疏检索的速度-空间权衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates how to substantially reduce the memory footprint of learned sparse retrieval indexes without sacrificing the efficiency of state-of-the-art retrieval data structures. Building on SEISMIC, we revisit both levels of its design: the inverted index used to select candidates and the forward index used to score them. For the inverted index, we replace costly per-block summaries with medoids, namely existing documents elected as block representatives, collapsing the per-block metadata from a sparse vector to a single document identifier. For the forward index, we compress both components and values. We reorder the vocabulary to place co-occurring components closer together and encode the resulting $Δ$-gaps with DOTPACKING8, a SIMD-friendly bit-packing scheme that fuses decompression with dot-product evaluation; values are quantized with compact per-component 4-bit codebooks fitted to each component's distribution. We further introduce JUMPDOT, a blocked dot-product kernel tailored for queries that contain only a few non-zero entries. Our forward-index compression is independent of SEISMIC and can be plugged into any system relying on forward-index-based scoring, as we demonstrate by integrating it into KANNOLO. A comprehensive evaluation on MS MARCO with three state-of-the-art learned sparse encoders shows that our solutions markedly improve the speed-space trade-off of learned sparse retrieval: at equal accuracy, our indexes answer queries up to 5.3x faster than the best competitor while using about 3x less memory, and in the most memory-constrained regime, they remain up to 1.9x faster while using up to 3.9x less memory.

</details>
